=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\public\script.js ===
/* ============================================================================
 * Forum — Frontend logic (vanilla JS, no frameworks)
 * ==========================================================================*/

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Helpers
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function escapeHtml(value) {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return '';
  return String(value)
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
    .replace(/'/g, '&#39;')
    .replace(/\//g, '&#x2F;');
}

function getCookie(name) {
  const m = document.cookie.match(new RegExp('(?:^|; )' + name + '=([^;]*)'));
  return m ? decodeURIComponent(m[1]) : null;
}

async function api(url, options = {}) {
  const opts = { ...options, headers: { ...(options.headers || {}) } };

  if (opts.body && typeof opts.body === 'object' && !(opts.body instanceof FormData)) {
    opts.headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
    opts.body = JSON.stringify(opts.body);
  }

  const method = (opts.method || 'GET').toUpperCase();
  if (!['GET', 'HEAD', 'OPTIONS'].includes(method)) {
    const csrf = getCookie('csrf_token');
    if (csrf) opts.headers['x-csrf-token'] = csrf;
  }

  const res = await fetch(url, opts);
  const ct = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
  const body = ct.includes('application/json') ? await res.json() : await res.text();
  return { ok: res.ok, status: res.status, body };
}

function setMessage(msg) {
  document.getElementById('message').textContent = msg || '';
}

async function withLoading(button, fn) {
  if (button) button.disabled = true;
  try {
    return await fn();
  } finally {
    if (button) button.disabled = false;
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// State
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const state = {
  filter: { type: 'all' },
  page: 1,
  pageSize: 10,
  isLoggedIn: false,
  username: null,
  userId: null,
  role: 'user',
};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Session
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async function checkSession() {
  const { ok, body } = await api('/me');
  state.isLoggedIn = !!(ok && body.loggedIn);
  state.username = state.isLoggedIn ? body.username : null;
  state.userId = state.isLoggedIn ? body.id : null;
  state.role = state.isLoggedIn ? body.role : 'user';

  const statusBox = document.getElementById('user-status');
  const authForms = document.getElementById('auth-forms');
  const userActions = document.getElementById('user-actions');
  const modSection = document.getElementById('moderation-section');
  const notifBell = document.getElementById('notif-bell');
  const notifDropdown = document.getElementById('notif-dropdown');
  const activitySection = document.getElementById('activity-section');

  if (state.isLoggedIn) {
    statusBox.textContent = `Welcome, ${state.username}! (${state.role})`;
    statusBox.style.background = '#d4edda';
    statusBox.style.color = '#155724';
    authForms.classList.add('hidden');
    userActions.classList.remove('hidden');
    document.querySelectorAll('.user-only').forEach((el) => el.classList.remove('hidden'));

    notifBell.classList.remove('hidden');
    activitySection.classList.remove('hidden');
    loadNotifications();
    loadActivity();

    if (state.role === 'admin' || state.role === 'moderator') {
      modSection.classList.remove('hidden');
      loadModerationDashboard();
    } else {
      modSection.classList.add('hidden');
    }
  } else {
    statusBox.textContent = 'You are browsing as a guest.';
    statusBox.style.background = '#f8d7da';
    statusBox.style.color = '#721c24';
    authForms.classList.remove('hidden');
    userActions.classList.add('hidden');
    modSection.classList.add('hidden');
    notifBell.classList.add('hidden');
    notifDropdown.classList.add('hidden');
    activitySection.classList.add('hidden');
    document.querySelectorAll('.user-only').forEach((el) => el.classList.add('hidden'));
  }

  await loadCategoryCheckboxes();
  await loadFilterCategories();
  await loadPosts();
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Auth
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  document.getElementById('login-btn').addEventListener('click', (e) =>
    withLoading(e.currentTarget, login)
  );
  document.getElementById('register-btn').addEventListener('click', (e) =>
    withLoading(e.currentTarget, register)
  );
  document.getElementById('logout-btn').addEventListener('click', (e) =>
    withLoading(e.currentTarget, logout)
  );
  document.getElementById('create-post-btn').addEventListener('click', (e) =>
    withLoading(e.currentTarget, createPost)
  );

  document.getElementById('filter-all')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    state.filter = { type: 'all' };
    state.page = 1;
    updateFilterButtons();
    loadPosts();
  });
  document.getElementById('filter-my-posts')?.addEventListener('click', filterByMyPosts);
  document.getElementById('filter-my-likes')?.addEventListener('click', filterByMyLikes);

  checkSession();
  loadSsoProviders();
});

async function login() {
  const username = document.getElementById('login-username').value.trim();
  const password = document.getElementById('login-password').value;
  const { ok, body } = await api('/login', { method: 'POST', body: { username, password } });
  if (ok) {
    setMessage('Login successful');
    document.getElementById('login-password').value = '';
    await checkSession();
  } else {
    setMessage(body.error || 'Login failed');
  }
}

async function register() {
  const email = document.getElementById('reg-email').value.trim();
  const username = document.getElementById('reg-username').value.trim();
  const password = document.getElementById('reg-password').value;
  const { ok, body } = await api('/register', {
    method: 'POST',
    body: { username, email, password },
  });
  if (ok) {
    setMessage('Registration successful — you can log in now');
    document.getElementById('reg-email').value = '';
    document.getElementById('reg-username').value = '';
    document.getElementById('reg-password').value = '';
  } else {
    setMessage(body.error || 'Registration failed');
  }
}

async function logout() {
  await api('/logout', { method: 'POST' });
  state.filter = { type: 'all' };
  state.page = 1;
  setMessage('');
  await checkSession();
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// SSO
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async function loadSsoProviders() {
  try {
    const { ok, body } = await api('/auth/providers');
    if (!ok) return;
    const container = document.getElementById('sso-buttons');
    const hint = document.getElementById('sso-hint');
    if (!container) return;
    let anyEnabled = false;
    for (const [name, enabled] of Object.entries(body.providers || {})) {
      const btn = container.querySelector(`[data-provider="${name}"]`);
      if (!btn || !enabled) continue;
      btn.hidden = false;
      anyEnabled = true;
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const redirect = encodeURIComponent(window.location.pathname || '/');
        window.location.href = `/auth/${name}?redirect_to=${redirect}`;
      });
    }
    if (!anyEnabled) hint.classList.remove('hidden');
  } catch (_) {}
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Categories
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async function loadCategoryCheckboxes() {
  const { ok, body } = await api('/api/categories');
  if (!ok) return;
  const container = document.getElementById('categories-container');
  container.innerHTML = '<p style="margin-bottom:5px;">Select Categories:</p>';
  for (const cat of body) {
    const label = document.createElement('label');
    label.className = 'category-checkbox';
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'checkbox';
    input.value = cat.id;
    label.appendChild(input);
    label.appendChild(document.createTextNode(' ' + cat.name));
    container.appendChild(label);
  }
}

async function loadFilterCategories() {
  const { ok, body } = await api('/api/categories');
  if (!ok) return;
  const container = document.getElementById('filter-categories-container');
  container.innerHTML = '';
  for (const cat of body) {
    const btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.type = 'button';
    btn.className = 'filter-cat-btn';
    btn.textContent = cat.name;
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => filterByCategory(cat.name, btn));
    container.appendChild(btn);
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Posts
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async function loadPosts() {
  const params = new URLSearchParams();
  if (state.filter.type === 'category') params.append('category', state.filter.value);
  if (state.filter.type === 'my_posts') params.append('filter', 'my_posts');
  if (state.filter.type === 'my_likes') params.append('filter', 'my_likes');
  params.append('page', state.page);
  params.append('pageSize', state.pageSize);

  const { ok, body } = await api('/api/posts?' + params.toString());
  const container = document.getElementById('posts-list');
  container.innerHTML = '';

  if (!ok) {
    container.innerHTML = `<p style="text-align:center; color:#c0392b;">${escapeHtml(body.error || 'Failed to load posts')}</p>`;
    document.getElementById('pagination').innerHTML = '';
    return;
  }
  if (body.data.length === 0) {
    container.innerHTML = '<p style="text-align:center; color:#7f8c8d;">No posts found.</p>';
    document.getElementById('pagination').innerHTML = '';
    return;
  }

  for (const post of body.data) {
    const div = document.createElement('div');
    div.className = 'post-card';
    const isOwner = state.isLoggedIn && post.user_id === state.userId;
    const isOther = state.isLoggedIn && post.user_id !== state.userId;

    div.innerHTML = `
      <h4>${escapeHtml(post.title)}</h4>
      <div class="meta">By ${escapeHtml(post.author)} | ${escapeHtml(new Date(post.created_at).toLocaleString())}</div>
      <p>${escapeHtml(post.content)}</p>
      <div class="categories">Categories: ${escapeHtml((post.categories || []).join(', ') || 'General')}</div>
      ${isOwner ? `
        <div class="post-actions">
          <button type="button" class="btn-edit-post" data-edit-post="${post.id}">Edit</button>
          <button type="button" class="btn-delete-post" data-delete-post="${post.id}">Delete</button>
          <button type="button" class="btn-add-image" data-add-image="${post.id}">Add Image</button>
          <input type="file" id="add-image-${post.id}" accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp,image/gif" style="display:none;" data-add-image-input="${post.id}" />
        </div>
      ` : ''}
      ${isOther ? `
        <div class="post-actions">
          <button type="button" class="btn-report-post" data-report-post="${post.id}" style="background: #e74c3c;">Report</button>
        </div>
      ` : ''}
      <div id="media-${post.id}" class="post-media"></div>
      <div class="likes-section">
        <button type="button" class="like-btn" data-action="like" data-post="${post.id}">Like (${post.likes_count || 0})</button>
        <button type="button" class="dislike-btn" data-action="dislike" data-post="${post.id}">Dislike (${post.dislikes_count || 0})</button>
      </div>
      <button type="button" class="comments-toggle-btn" data-toggle-comments="${post.id}">Comments (${post.comments_count || 0})</button>
      <div id="comments-section-${post.id}" class="comments-section" style="display:none;">
        <div id="comments-${post.id}"></div>
        ${state.isLoggedIn ? `
          <textarea id="comment-input-${post.id}" class="comment-input" placeholder="Write a comment..." rows="2" maxlength="2000"></textarea>
          <button type="button" class="add-comment-btn" data-add-comment="${post.id}">Add Comment</button>
        ` : '<p style="color:#7f8c8d; font-size:.9em; text-align:center; margin-top:10px;">Login to comment</p>'}
      </div>
    `;
    container.appendChild(div);
  }

  container.querySelectorAll('[data-action][data-post]').forEach((btn) => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', (e) =>
      withLoading(e.currentTarget, () => reactToPost(btn.dataset.post, btn.dataset.action))
    );
  });

  container.querySelectorAll('[data-toggle-comments]').forEach((btn) => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => toggleComments(btn.dataset.toggleComments));
  });

  container.querySelectorAll('[data-add-comment]').forEach((btn) => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', (e) =>
      withLoading(e.currentTarget, () => addComment(btn.dataset.addComment))
    );
  });

  container.querySelectorAll('[data-edit-post]').forEach((btn) => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => openEditModal(btn.dataset.editPost));
  });
  container.querySelectorAll('[data-delete-post]').forEach((btn) => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => deletePost(btn.dataset.deletePost));
  });

  container.querySelectorAll('[data-add-image]').forEach((btn) => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      document.getElementById(`add-image-${btn.dataset.addImage}`).click();
    });
  });
  container.querySelectorAll('[data-add-image-input]').forEach((input) => {
    input.addEventListener('change', async (e) => {
      if (!e.target.files.length) return;
      const postId = input.dataset.addImageInput;
      const formData = new FormData();
      formData.append('file', e.target.files[0]);
      const res = await api(`/api/posts/${postId}/media`, { method: 'POST', body: formData });
      if (res.ok) {
        setMessage('Image added successfully!');
        await loadPostMedia(postId);
      } else {
        setMessage(res.body.error || 'Failed to add image');
      }
      e.target.value = '';
    });
  });

  container.querySelectorAll('[data-report-post]').forEach((btn) => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      openReportModal('post', parseInt(btn.dataset.reportPost, 10));
    });
  });

  for (const post of body.data) {
    loadPostMedia(post.id).catch(() => {});
  }

  renderPagination(body.pagination);
}

function renderPagination({ page, pageSize, total }) {
  const container = document.getElementById('pagination');
  container.innerHTML = '';
  const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / pageSize));
  if (pages <= 1) return;
  for (let i = 1; i <= pages; i++) {
    const b = document.createElement('button');
    b.type = 'button';
    b.textContent = String(i);
    if (i === page) b.classList.add('active');
    b.addEventListener('click', () => { state.page = i; loadPosts(); });
    container.appendChild(b);
  }
}

async function createPost() {
  const title = document.getElementById('post-title').value.trim();
  const content = document.getElementById('post-content').value.trim();
  const categoryIds = Array.from(
    document.querySelectorAll('#categories-container input[type="checkbox"]:checked')
  ).map((cb) => parseInt(cb.value, 10));

  if (!title || !content || categoryIds.length === 0) {
    setMessage('Please fill all fields and select at least one category.');
    return;
  }

  const { ok, body } = await api('/api/posts', {
    method: 'POST',
    body: { title, content, categoryIds },
  });

  if (ok) {
    setMessage('Post created successfully!');
    document.getElementById('post-title').value = '';
    document.getElementById('post-content').value = '';
    document.querySelectorAll('#categories-container input:checked').forEach((c) => (c.checked = false));

    const fileInput = document.getElementById('post-media');
    if (fileInput && fileInput.files.length > 0) {
      const formData = new FormData();
      formData.append('file', fileInput.files[0]);
      const mediaRes = await api(`/api/posts/${body.id}/media`, {
        method: 'POST',
        body: formData,
      });
      if (mediaRes.ok) {
        setMessage('Post and Media uploaded successfully!');
      } else {
        setMessage('Post created, but media upload failed: ' + (mediaRes.body.error || ''));
      }
      fileInput.value = '';
    }
    state.page = 1;
    await loadPosts();
  } else {
    setMessage(body.error || 'Failed to create post');
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Reactions
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async function reactToPost(postId, action) {
  const { ok, body } = await api(`/api/posts/${postId}/${action}`, { method: 'POST' });
  if (!ok) { setMessage(body.error || 'Failed'); return; }
  await loadPosts();
}

async function reactToComment(commentId, action, postId) {
  const { ok, body } = await api(`/api/comments/${commentId}/${action}`, { method: 'POST' });
  if (!ok) { setMessage(body.error || 'Failed'); return; }
  await loadComments(postId);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Comments
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async function toggleComments(postId) {
  const section = document.getElementById(`comments-section-${postId}`);
  if (section.style.display === 'none') {
    section.style.display = 'block';
    await loadComments(postId);
  } else {
    section.style.display = 'none';
  }
}

async function loadComments(postId) {
  const { ok, body } = await api(`/api/posts/${postId}/comments`);
  const container = document.getElementById(`comments-${postId}`);
  if (!container) return;
  container.innerHTML = '';
  if (!ok) {
    container.innerHTML = `<p style="color:#c0392b;">${escapeHtml(body.error || 'Failed to load comments')}</p>`;
    return;
  }
  if (body.length === 0) {
    container.innerHTML = '<p style="color:#7f8c8d; font-size:.9em; text-align:center;">No comments yet.</p>';
    return;
  }

  for (const c of body) {
    const div = document.createElement('div');
    div.className = 'comment-card';
    const isOther = state.isLoggedIn && c.user_id !== state.userId;

    div.innerHTML = `
      <span class="comment-author">${escapeHtml(c.author)}</span>
      <span class="comment-date">${escapeHtml(new Date(c.created_at).toLocaleString())}</span>
      <p style="margin:5px 0;">${escapeHtml(c.content)}</p>
      <div class="likes-section">
        <button type="button" class="like-btn" data-caction="like" data-comment="${c.id}" data-post="${postId}">Like (${c.likes_count || 0})</button>
        <button type="button" class="dislike-btn" data-caction="dislike" data-comment="${c.id}" data-post="${postId}">Dislike (${c.dislikes_count || 0})</button>
        ${isOther ? `<button type="button" class="btn-report-comment" data-report-comment="${c.id}" style="background: #e74c3c; flex: 0; padding: 8px 12px; font-size: 12px; width: auto;">Report</button>` : ''}
      </div>
    `;
    container.appendChild(div);
  }

  container.querySelectorAll('[data-caction]').forEach((btn) => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', (e) =>
      withLoading(e.currentTarget, () => reactToComment(btn.dataset.comment, btn.dataset.caction, btn.dataset.post))
    );
  });

  container.querySelectorAll('[data-report-comment]').forEach((btn) => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      openReportModal('comment', parseInt(btn.dataset.reportComment, 10));
    });
  });
}

async function addComment(postId) {
  const input = document.getElementById(`comment-input-${postId}`);
  const content = input.value.trim();
  if (!content) { setMessage('Comment cannot be empty.'); return; }
  const { ok, body } = await api(`/api/posts/${postId}/comments`, { method: 'POST', body: { content } });
  if (ok) {
    input.value = '';
    await loadComments(postId);
    await loadPosts();
  } else {
    setMessage(body.error || 'Failed to add comment');
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Media
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async function loadPostMedia(postId) {
  const { ok, body } = await api(`/api/posts/${postId}/media`);
  if (!ok || !Array.isArray(body)) return;
  const container = document.getElementById(`media-${postId}`);
  if (!container) return;
  container.innerHTML = '';
  for (const m of body) {
    const img = document.createElement('img');
    img.src = m.url;
    img.alt = 'Post media';
    img.loading = 'lazy';
    container.appendChild(img);
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Filters
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function filterByMyPosts() {
  state.filter = { type: 'my_posts' };
  state.page = 1;
  updateFilterButtons();
  loadPosts();
}

function filterByMyLikes() {
  state.filter = { type: 'my_likes' };
  state.page = 1;
  updateFilterButtons();
  loadPosts();
}

function filterByCategory(categoryName, btnElement) {
  state.filter = { type: 'category', value: categoryName };
  state.page = 1;
  updateFilterButtons();
  document.querySelectorAll('.filter-cat-btn').forEach((b) => b.classList.remove('active'));
  btnElement.classList.add('active');
  loadPosts();
}

function updateFilterButtons() {
  document.querySelectorAll('.filter-btn').forEach((b) => b.classList.remove('active'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.filter-cat-btn').forEach((b) => b.classList.remove('active'));
  const btns = document.querySelectorAll('.filter-btn');
  if (state.filter.type === 'all' && btns[0]) btns[0].classList.add('active');
  if (state.filter.type === 'my_posts' && btns[1]) btns[1].classList.add('active');
  if (state.filter.type === 'my_likes' && btns[2]) btns[2].classList.add('active');
}

// ============================================================================
// Moderation Dashboard (F05)
// ============================================================================
async function loadModerationDashboard() {
  const { ok, body } = await api('/api/moderation/queue');
  if (ok) {
    renderModList('mod-queue-posts', body.posts, 'post');
    renderModList('mod-queue-comments', body.comments, 'comment');
  }
  const rep = await api('/api/moderation/reports');
  if (rep.ok) renderReports(rep.body);
}

function renderModList(elementId, items, type) {
  const container = document.getElementById(elementId);
  container.innerHTML = '';
  if (items.length === 0) {
    container.innerHTML = '<p style="color:#7f8c8d; font-size:.9em;">Nothing pending.</p>';
    return;
  }
  items.forEach(item => {
    const div = document.createElement('div');
    div.className = 'mod-card';
    const title = type === 'post' ? item.title : item.content;
    div.innerHTML = `
      <h5>${escapeHtml(title)}</h5>
      <div class="meta">By ${escapeHtml(item.author)}</div>
      <div class="mod-actions">
        <button class="btn-approve" data-action="approved">Approve</button>
        <button class="btn-reject" data-action="rejected">Reject</button>
      </div>
    `;
    div.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => { updateModStatus(type, item.id, btn.dataset.action); });
    });
    container.appendChild(div);
  });
}

async function updateModStatus(type, id, status) {
  const url = `/api/moderation/${type}s/${id}/status`;
  const { ok } = await api(url, { method: 'PATCH', body: { status } });
  if (ok) {
    setMessage(`${type} ${status}`);
    loadModerationDashboard();
    if (type === 'post') loadPosts();
  }
}

function renderReports(reports) {
  const container = document.getElementById('mod-reports');
  container.innerHTML = '';
  if (reports.length === 0) {
    container.innerHTML = '<p style="color:#7f8c8d; font-size:.9em;">No open reports.</p>';
    return;
  }
  reports.forEach(r => {
    const div = document.createElement('div');
    div.className = 'mod-card';
    div.innerHTML = `
      <h5>Report #${r.id} (${r.target_type} #${r.target_id})</h5>
      <p>Reason: ${escapeHtml(r.reason)}</p>
      <div class="mod-actions">
        <button class="btn-resolve" data-action="resolved">Resolve</button>
        <button class="btn-reject" data-action="dismissed">Dismiss</button>
      </div>
    `;
    div.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
        const { ok } = await api(`/api/moderation/reports/${r.id}`, {
          method: 'PATCH', body: { status: btn.dataset.action }
        });
        if (ok) loadModerationDashboard();
      });
    });
    container.appendChild(div);
  });
}

// ============================================================================
// Advanced Features (F06)
// ============================================================================
async function loadNotifications() {
  const { ok, body } = await api('/api/notifications');
  if (!ok) return;
  const unread = body.filter(n => !n.is_read).length;
  const badge = document.getElementById('notif-badge');
  if (unread > 0) {
    badge.textContent = unread;
    badge.classList.remove('hidden');
  } else {
    badge.classList.add('hidden');
  }
  const list = document.getElementById('notif-list');
  list.innerHTML = body.length === 0
    ? '<p style="color:#7f8c8d; font-size:.9em;">No notifications yet.</p>'
    : '';
  const icons = { like: '👍', comment: '💬', reply: '↩️' };
  body.forEach(n => {
    const div = document.createElement('div');
    div.className = `notif-item ${n.is_read ? '' : 'unread'}`;
    div.innerHTML = `
      <span>${icons[n.type] || '🔔'}</span>
      <strong>${escapeHtml(n.actor_name)}</strong>
      ${n.type === 'like' ? 'liked your' : 'commented on your'} ${n.target_type}
      <div style="font-size:.8em; color:#7f8c8d;">${new Date(n.created_at).toLocaleString()}</div>
    `;
    div.addEventListener('click', async () => {
      await api(`/api/notifications/${n.id}/read`, { method: 'PATCH' });
      loadNotifications();
    });
    list.appendChild(div);
  });
}

let editingPost = null;

async function openEditModal(postId) {
  const { ok, body } = await api(`/api/posts/${postId}`);
  if (!ok) {
    const listRes = await api('/api/posts?page=1&pageSize=100');
    if (!listRes.ok) return;
    editingPost = listRes.body.data.find(p => p.id === parseInt(postId, 10));
  } else {
    editingPost = body;
  }
  if (!editingPost) return;
  document.getElementById('edit-title').value = editingPost.title;
  document.getElementById('edit-content').value = editingPost.content;
  document.getElementById('edit-error').textContent = '';
  const catContainer = document.getElementById('edit-categories-container');
  catContainer.innerHTML = '<p style="margin-bottom:5px;">Categories:</p>';
  const catRes = await api('/api/categories');
  if (catRes.ok) {
    catRes.body.forEach(cat => {
      const label = document.createElement('label');
      label.className = 'category-checkbox';
      const checked = editingPost.categories.includes(cat.name) ? 'checked' : '';
      label.innerHTML = `<input type="checkbox" value="${cat.id}" ${checked}> ${cat.name}`;
      catContainer.appendChild(label);
    });
  }
  document.getElementById('edit-modal').classList.remove('hidden');
}

async function saveEdit() {
  if (!editingPost) return;
  const title = document.getElementById('edit-title').value.trim();
  const content = document.getElementById('edit-content').value.trim();
  const categoryIds = Array.from(
    document.querySelectorAll('#edit-categories-container input:checked')
  ).map(cb => parseInt(cb.value, 10));
  const { ok, body } = await api(`/api/posts/${editingPost.id}`, {
    method: 'PUT',
    body: { title, content, categoryIds, updatedAt: editingPost.updated_at }
  });
  if (ok) {
    document.getElementById('edit-modal').classList.add('hidden');
    editingPost = null;
    await loadPosts();
  } else {
    document.getElementById('edit-error').textContent = body.error || 'Failed to update';
  }
}

async function deletePost(postId) {
  if (!confirm('Delete this post? This cannot be undone.')) return;
  const { ok, body } = await api(`/api/posts/${postId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
  if (ok) { setMessage('Post deleted'); await loadPosts(); }
  else { alert(body.error || 'Failed to delete'); }
}

// --- F05: Report Content Modal ---
let reportTarget = { type: null, id: null };

function openReportModal(type, id) {
  reportTarget = { type, id };
  document.getElementById('report-reason').value = '';
  document.getElementById('report-error').textContent = '';
  document.getElementById('report-modal').classList.remove('hidden');
}

function closeReportModal() {
  document.getElementById('report-modal').classList.add('hidden');
  reportTarget = { type: null, id: null };
}

async function submitReport() {
  if (!reportTarget.type) return;
  const reason = document.getElementById('report-reason').value.trim();
  if (reason.length < 3) {
    document.getElementById('report-error').textContent = 'Reason must be at least 3 characters.';
    return;
  }
  const { ok, body } = await api('/api/reports', {
    method: 'POST',
    body: { targetType: reportTarget.type, targetId: reportTarget.id, reason },
  });
  if (ok) {
    closeReportModal();
    setMessage('Content reported successfully. Moderators will review it.');
  } else {
    document.getElementById('report-error').textContent = body.error || 'Failed to report';
  }
}

// --- F04: Change Password ---
async function submitPasswordChange() {
  const currentPassword = document.getElementById('current-password').value;
  const newPassword = document.getElementById('new-password').value;
  const errorEl = document.getElementById('password-error');
  if (!currentPassword || !newPassword) { errorEl.textContent = 'All fields are required'; return; }
  if (newPassword.length < 8 || newPassword.length > 72) { errorEl.textContent = 'New password must be 8-72 characters'; return; }
  if (currentPassword === newPassword) { errorEl.textContent = 'New password must differ from the current one'; return; }
  const { ok, body } = await api('/api/users/me/password', {
    method: 'POST',
    body: { currentPassword, newPassword },
  });
  if (ok) {
    document.getElementById('password-modal').classList.add('hidden');
    document.getElementById('current-password').value = '';
    document.getElementById('new-password').value = '';
    setMessage('Password updated successfully!');
  } else {
    errorEl.textContent = body.error || 'Failed to update password';
  }
}

// --- F06: Activity Feed ---
async function loadActivity() {
  const { ok, body } = await api('/api/activity/me');
  if (!ok) return;
  const container = document.getElementById('activity-list');
  container.innerHTML = body.length === 0 ? '<p style="color:#7f8c8d;">No activity yet.</p>' : '';
  const labels = {
    post_create: '📝 Created a post',
    comment_create: '💬 Commented',
    post_edit: '✏️ Edited a post',
    comment_edit: '✏️ Edited a comment',
  };
  body.forEach(a => {
    const div = document.createElement('div');
    div.className = 'activity-item';
    div.innerHTML = `
      ${labels[a.action] || a.action} (${a.target_type} #${a.target_id})
      <span style="float:right; font-size:.85em; color:#7f8c8d;">${new Date(a.created_at).toLocaleString()}</span>
    `;
    container.appendChild(div);
  });
}

// --- F06: Wire up listeners ---
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  document.getElementById('notif-bell')?.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    if (e.target.closest('#notif-dropdown')) return;
    const dd = document.getElementById('notif-dropdown');
    dd.classList.toggle('hidden');
    if (!dd.classList.contains('hidden')) loadNotifications();
  });

  document.getElementById('edit-save-btn')?.addEventListener('click', (e) =>
    withLoading(e.currentTarget, saveEdit));
  document.getElementById('edit-cancel-btn')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    document.getElementById('edit-modal').classList.add('hidden');
    editingPost = null;
  });

  document.getElementById('change-password-btn')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    document.getElementById('password-modal').classList.remove('hidden');
    document.getElementById('password-error').textContent = '';
    document.getElementById('current-password').value = '';
    document.getElementById('new-password').value = '';
  });
  document.getElementById('cancel-password-btn')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    document.getElementById('password-modal').classList.add('hidden');
    document.getElementById('current-password').value = '';
    document.getElementById('new-password').value = '';
  });
  document.getElementById('save-password-btn')?.addEventListener('click', (e) =>
    withLoading(e.currentTarget, submitPasswordChange));

  document.getElementById('report-submit-btn')?.addEventListener('click', (e) =>
    withLoading(e.currentTarget, submitReport));
  document.getElementById('report-cancel-btn')?.addEventListener('click', closeReportModal);

  setInterval(() => {
    if (state.isLoggedIn) loadNotifications();
  }, 30000);
});

=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\README.md ===
# Forum Project — F01 → F06 Complete Track (+ P21 Bonus)

> A production-grade, full-stack community forum built with **Node.js, Express 5, and SQLCipher-encrypted SQLite**. This project implements the complete Anas Roadmap (P01–P26 + F01–F06), featuring enterprise-level authentication, hardened security, verified media uploads, comprehensive moderation workflows, advanced user features, and **live database encryption**.

[![Node](https://img.shields.io/badge/node-%3E%3D22-brightgreen)](https://nodejs.org/)
[![SQLite](https://img.shields.io/badge/sqlite-3%20%2B%20SQLCipher-blue)](https://www.zetetic.net/sqlcipher/)
[![Express](https://img.shields.io/badge/express-5-lightgrey)](https://expressjs.com/)
[![Tests](https://img.shields.io/badge/tests-107%2F107%20passing-success)](#running-tests)
[![License](https://img.shields.io/badge/license-ISC-lightgrey)](./LICENSE)

---

## نبذة سريعة (Arabic Quick Intro)

مشروع منتدى متكامل يغطي المسار الكامل من P01 إلى P26 ثم F01 إلى F06، بما في ذلك بند P21 (تشفير قاعدة البيانات). يبدأ من الأساسيات، ويمر عبر SSO، رفع الصور، تقوية الجلسات، الإشراف، الميزات المتقدمة، وينتهي بقاعدة بيانات مشفّرة بـ SQLCipher.

---

## Features Overview

### F01 — Base Forum

- **Auth**: Register, Login, Logout. bcrypt (cost 12).
- **Sessions**: Server-side sessions (UUID-based). HttpOnly, SameSite=Lax.
- **Community**: Posts (with categories), comments, reactions (likes/dislikes).
- **Filters**: By category, "My Posts", "My Likes". Pagination.
- **Security**: CSRF, XSS escapeHtml, SQLi prepared statements, Rate Limiting.

### F02 — SSO Authentication (Google & GitHub)

- **Google OIDC**: Authorization Code + PKCE (S256) + nonce.
- **GitHub OAuth2**: Raw OAuth2 + PKCE + primary email resolution.
- **Account Linking**: Strict rejection of unverified emails (409).

### F03 — Verified Media Upload

- **UI**: File input on post creation + "Add Image" button on owned posts.
- **Streaming Intake**: busboy (5MB cap).
- **Magic Byte Detection**: file-type (no MIME spoofing).
- **Processing**: sharp decode/re-encode (destroys polyglots/RCE, strips EXIF).
- **Storage**: SHA-256 addressed, sharded, atomic writes.

### F04 — Session Hardening & Security

- **UI**: Change Password modal (session rotation after change).
- **Session Rotation**: New UUID on login/password change.
- **Global Logout**: Single-session policy.
- **UA Binding**: Session tied to User-Agent hash.
- **Timeouts**: Idle 2h / Absolute 7d.
- **Docker Hardening**: Read-only FS, tmpfs, non-root, cap_drop: ALL.
- **TLS 1.3**: Mozilla Intermediate profile.

### F05 — Moderation & Roles

- **UI**: "Report" button on every post/comment (non-owner) + mod dashboard.
- **RBAC**: `user`, `moderator`, `admin`.
- **Bootstrapping**: First registered user = admin.
- **Approval Queue**: Moderators approve/reject pending content.
- **Reports**: User reports, moderator resolves/dismisses.

### F06 — Advanced Features

- **Owned Editing**: Users edit/delete their own content.
- **Optimistic Locking**: `updated_at` check → 409 on stale edits.
- **Notifications**: Bell icon + unread count, polled.
- **Activity Logs**: Personal history of creations/edits.

### P21 — Live Database Encryption (Bonus)

- **Encryption at rest**: SQLCipher via `better-sqlite3-multiple-ciphers`.
- **Password-driven**: Set `DB_PASSWORD` in `.env` to enable.
- **Backup**: `npm run db:backup` → encrypted backup + manifest.
- **Restore**: `npm run db:restore <path>` → verified restore.
- **Tests**: `tests/f04.db-encryption.test.js` (5 tests).

---

## Tech Stack

| Layer | Technology |
| --- | --- |
| Runtime | Node.js 22 (LTS) |
| Web framework | Express 5 |
| Database | SQLCipher (`better-sqlite3-multiple-ciphers`) |
| Password Hashing | `bcryptjs` (cost 12) |
| Sessions | `cookie-session` (signed) + server-side store |
| OIDC/OAuth | `openid-client` v6 |
| Multipart Intake | `busboy` v1 |
| Image Processing | `sharp` v0.35 |
| File Detection | `file-type` v16 (CJS) |
| Rate Limiting | `express-rate-limit` v8 (IPv6-safe) |
| Tests | Jest 30 + Supertest 7 |
| Container | Docker (Alpine, multi-stage, non-root) |

---

---

## Database Schema

Managed by `src/database/schema.js`. Key tables:

- `users`: `role` (user/mod/admin)
- `sessions`: `last_seen_at`, `user_agent_hash` (F04)
- `identities`: Google/GitHub links (F02)
- `posts` & `comments`: `status`, `updated_at` (F06)
- `reactions`: Unified likes/dislikes
- `media`: SHA-256 addressed images (F03)
- `reports`: Moderation queue (F05)
- `notifications` & `activity_logs`: F06

*(See `docs/ERD.md` for the full Mermaid diagram).*

---

## Installation & Running

### Prerequisites

- Node.js ≥ 22
- Docker (optional, required for F04 hardening and P21 demonstration)

### Local Development (with encryption)

```bash
npm install

cp .env.example .env

node -e "console.log('SESSION_SECRET=' + require('crypto').randomBytes(48).toString('hex'))"
node -e "console.log('CSRF_SECRET=' + require('crypto').randomBytes(48).toString('hex'))"
node -e "console.log('DB_PASSWORD=' + require('crypto').randomBytes(32).toString('hex'))"

# Add the three values to .env (P21 is now active)

npm run db:seed
npm run dev
```

Open `https://localhost:3000` (accept self-signed cert).

### Local Development (without encryption, for quick testing)

Leave `DB_PASSWORD=` empty in `.env`. Everything works exactly like before P21.

### Docker Setup (Recommended)

```bash
docker compose up --build -d
docker compose ps   # wait for "healthy"
```

Open `https://localhost:3000`.

> **First-time login:** First registered user becomes `admin`.

---

## P21 — Database Encryption (Bonus)

### Enable encryption

Add to `.env`:

```env
DB_PASSWORD=your-strong-password-here
```

### Backup (creates an encrypted copy)

```bash
npm run db:backup
# or with a custom path:
npm run db:backup -- ./data/backup-2026-01-01.db
```

### Restore (verify key, then replace live DB)

```bash
# Stop the server first
npm run db:restore -- ./data/backup-2026-01-01.db
```

### Verify encryption

```bash
# Raw bytes should NOT start with "SQLite format 3"
head -c 16 data/forum.db | od -c
```

---

## Running Tests

```bash
npm test             # 18 suites, 107 tests (includes P21)
npm test -- f04      # specific feature
npm test -- db-encryption  # P21 only
```

---

## Security Highlights

- **XSS**: Output encoding + strict CSP headers.
- **CSRF**: HMAC-signed double-submit cookies.
- **SQLi**: 100% prepared statements.
- **Session Hijacking**: User-Agent binding + IP-based rate limits.
- **Brute Force**: Per-username login limits (regardless of IP).
- **Account Takeover**: Verified email required for SSO linking.
- **RCE via Uploads**: Decode + re-encode destroys payloads.
- **DoS**: 10kb body limit, 5MB upload cap, strict timeouts.
- **Data at rest (P21)**: SQLCipher encryption with `DB_PASSWORD`.

---

=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\scripts\backup-db.js ===
// ============================================================================
// scripts/backup-db.js — Create an encrypted backup of the SQLCipher database
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Usage: npm run db:backup [-- ./data/backup-YYYYMMDD.db]
//
// The backup is encrypted with the SAME password as the live DB (DB_PASSWORD).
// Use `sqlcipher_export()` to produce a portable copy.
// ============================================================================

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const Database = require('better-sqlite3-multiple-ciphers');

const config = require('../src/config');
const logger = require('../src/utils/logger');

function main() {
  if (!config.db.password) {
    console.error('DB_PASSWORD is not set. Cannot create an encrypted backup.');
    process.exit(1);
  }

  if (config.db.path === ':memory:') {
    console.error('Cannot back up an in-memory database.');
    process.exit(1);
  }

  const dbPath = path.resolve(config.db.path);
  const backupPath = path.resolve(
    process.argv[2] || `./data/backup-${Date.now()}.db`
  );

  if (!fs.existsSync(dbPath)) {
    console.error(`Database not found: ${dbPath}`);
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // Make sure the backup directory exists
  const backupDir = path.dirname(backupPath);
  if (!fs.existsSync(backupDir)) {
    fs.mkdirSync(backupDir, { recursive: true });
  }

  // Remove any stale backup
  if (fs.existsSync(backupPath)) {
    fs.unlinkSync(backupPath);
  }

  // Open the source DB with the correct key
  const db = new Database(dbPath);
  db.pragma(`key = '${config.db.password}'`);

  // Verify key by reading
  try {
    db.prepare('SELECT count(*) AS c FROM sqlite_master').get();
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Wrong DB_PASSWORD (cannot read DB):', err.message);
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // Export to backup file (encrypted with the same password)
  db.exec(`ATTACH DATABASE '${backupPath}' AS backup KEY '${config.db.password}'`);
  db.exec('SELECT sqlcipher_export(\'backup\')');
  db.exec('DETACH DATABASE backup');
  db.close();

  const stats = fs.statSync(backupPath);
  logger.info(`✅ Backup created: ${backupPath} (${stats.size} bytes)`);

  // Also copy a plaintext manifest for reference
  const manifestPath = backupPath + '.manifest.json';
  fs.writeFileSync(
    manifestPath,
    JSON.stringify(
      {
        created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
        source_db: dbPath,
        source_size: fs.statSync(dbPath).size,
        backup_size: stats.size,
        encrypted: true,
      },
      null,
      2
    )
  );
  logger.info(`   Manifest: ${manifestPath}`);
}

try {
  main();
} catch (err) {
  console.error('Backup failed:', err);
  process.exit(1);
}


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\scripts\cleanup-media.js ===
// ============================================================================
// scripts/cleanup-media.js — Remove orphan media files
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// An "orphan" is a file on disk that has no corresponding row in the media
// table. This can happen if the DB row was deleted without the file, or if a
// crash interrupted the two-step delete.
//
// Usage: npm run media:cleanup
// ============================================================================

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

const db = require('../src/database/db');
const config = require('../src/config');
const logger = require('../src/utils/logger');

function walk(dir, out = []) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return out;

  for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    const full = path.join(dir, entry.name);

    if (entry.isDirectory()) {
      walk(full, out);
    } else if (entry.isFile() && !entry.name.endsWith('.tmp')) {
      out.push(full);
    }
  }

  return out;
}

function cleanup() {
  const root = path.resolve(config.media.root);

  if (!fs.existsSync(root)) {
    console.log(`Media root does not exist: ${root}`);
    return;
  }

  const known = new Set(
    db
      .prepare('SELECT storage_path FROM media')
      .all()
      .map((r) => path.resolve(root, r.storage_path))
  );

  let removed = 0;
  let kept = 0;

  for (const full of walk(root)) {
    if (known.has(full)) {
      kept++;
    } else {
      try {
        fs.unlinkSync(full);
        logger.info(`Removed orphan: ${full}`);
        removed++;
      } catch (err) {
        logger.error(`Failed to remove: ${full}`, err);
      }
    }
  }

  console.log('');
  console.log(`Media root: ${root}`);
  console.log(`Files kept:   ${kept}`);
  console.log(`Files removed: ${removed}`);
}

cleanup();

=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\scripts\restore-db.js ===
// ============================================================================
// scripts/restore-db.js — Restore the SQLCipher database from a backup
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Usage: npm run db:restore -- ./data/backup-YYYYMMDD.db
//
// Stops existing DB connections, verifies the backup key, then replaces the
// live DB file. Existing WAL/SHM files are removed to avoid corruption.
//
// IMPORTANT: Stop the server BEFORE running this script.
// ============================================================================

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const Database = require('better-sqlite3-multiple-ciphers');

const config = require('../src/config');
const logger = require('../src/utils/logger');

function main() {
  if (!config.db.password) {
    console.error('DB_PASSWORD is not set. Cannot restore an encrypted backup.');
    process.exit(1);
  }

  const backupPath = process.argv[2];
  if (!backupPath) {
    console.error('Usage: npm run db:restore -- <path-to-backup.db>');
    process.exit(1);
  }

  const backupAbs = path.resolve(backupPath);
  const dbPath = path.resolve(config.db.path);

  if (!fs.existsSync(backupAbs)) {
    console.error(`Backup file not found: ${backupAbs}`);
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // Verify backup key by opening and reading
  logger.info(`Verifying backup: ${backupAbs}`);
  const verifyDb = new Database(backupAbs);
  verifyDb.pragma(`key = '${config.db.password}'`);
  try {
    const tables = verifyDb.prepare(
      "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'"
    ).all();
    logger.info(`✅ Backup verified. Tables: ${tables.map((t) => t.name).join(', ')}`);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Wrong DB_PASSWORD (cannot read backup):', err.message);
    process.exit(1);
  }
  verifyDb.close();

  // Refuse if the target DB is currently open (best-effort check)
  if (fs.existsSync(dbPath)) {
    try {
      const testDb = new Database(dbPath, { readonly: true });
      testDb.close();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(
        'Target DB appears to be in use. Stop the server before restoring.'
      );
      process.exit(1);
    }
  }

  // Copy backup over DB
  fs.copyFileSync(backupAbs, dbPath);

  // Remove WAL/SHM
  for (const suffix of ['-wal', '-shm']) {
    const f = dbPath + suffix;
    if (fs.existsSync(f)) fs.unlinkSync(f);
  }

  logger.info(`✅ Restore complete. Target: ${dbPath}`);
}

try {
  main();
} catch (err) {
  console.error('Restore failed:', err);
  process.exit(1);
}
