// ============================================================================
// scripts/seed-demo-media.js — Attach a demo image to the first post
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Usage: node scripts/seed-demo-media.js
// Useful for visually testing the media pipeline in the browser.
// ============================================================================

const sharp = require('sharp');

const db = require('../src/database/db');
const { storeBuffer } = require('../src/media/storage');
const mediaModel = require('../src/models/media.model');

async function run() {
  const post = db.prepare('SELECT id, user_id FROM posts LIMIT 1').get();

  if (!post) {
    console.log('No posts found. Create a post first, then re-run this script.');
    process.exit(0);
  }

  console.log(`Attaching demo image to post id=${post.id} (owner=${post.user_id})...`);

  const buffer = await sharp({
    create: {
      width: 400,
      height: 300,
      channels: 3,
      background: { r: 100, g: 150, b: 200 },
    },
  })
    .webp()
    .toBuffer();

  const stored = await storeBuffer(buffer, 'webp');

  const media = mediaModel.create({
    userId: post.user_id,
    postId: post.id,
    kind: 'post_image',
    originalName: 'demo.webp',
    mimeType: 'image/webp',
    sizeBytes: buffer.length,
    width: 400,
    height: 300,
    storagePath: stored.relativePath,
    sha256: stored.sha256,
  });

  console.log(`✅ Created demo media id=${media.id}`);
  console.log(`   URL: /api/media/${media.id}`);
  console.log(`   Path: ${stored.relativePath}`);
  console.log('');
  console.log('Reload the browser to see the image attached to the post.');
}

run().catch((err) => {
  console.error('FAILED:', err);
  process.exit(1);
});


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\server.js ===
// ============================================================================
// server.js — Bootstrap entry point
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Order of operations is CRITICAL:
//   1. Run migrations (creates tables)
//   2. Seed default data
//   3. Ensure TLS certificates
//   4. Load the Express app (models can now prepare statements)
//   5. Start HTTPS server
// ============================================================================

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const https = require('https');

const config = require('./src/config');
const logger = require('./src/utils/logger');
const { runMigrations } = require('./src/database/schema');

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// TLS certificates
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async function ensureCertificates() {
  const { certPath, keyPath, days, commonName, country, state, locality, org } = config.tls;

  const certDir = path.dirname(certPath);
  if (!fs.existsSync(certDir)) fs.mkdirSync(certDir, { recursive: true });

  if (fs.existsSync(certPath) && fs.existsSync(keyPath)) {
    return;
  }

  logger.warn('TLS certificates not found — generating self-signed pair');

  const selfsigned = require('selfsigned');

  const attrs = [
    { name: 'CN', value: commonName },
    { name: 'C',  value: country },
    { name: 'ST', value: state },
    { name: 'L',  value: locality },
    { name: 'O',  value: org },
  ];

  const pems = await selfsigned.generate(attrs, {
    days,
    keySize: 2048,
    algorithm: 'sha256',
  });

  if (!pems || !pems.private || !pems.cert) {
    throw new Error('selfsigned.generate returned an unexpected shape');
  }

  fs.writeFileSync(keyPath, pems.private, { mode: 0o600 });
  fs.writeFileSync(certPath, pems.cert, { mode: 0o644 });

  logger.info('Self-signed certificates generated');
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Bootstrap
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async function bootstrap() {
  // 1. Migrations FIRST — before any model is loaded.
  try {
    runMigrations();
  } catch (err) {
    logger.error('Migrations failed — aborting startup', err);
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // 2. Seed default data (idempotent).
  try {
    const { seed } = require('./src/database/seed');
    seed();
  } catch (err) {
    logger.error('Seeding failed (continuing)', err);
  }

  // 3. TLS certificates.
  try {
    await ensureCertificates();
  } catch (err) {
    logger.error('Could not ensure TLS certificates — aborting startup', err);
    process.exit(1);
  }

  // 4. NOW load the app — models prepare statements against a ready schema.
  const app = require('./src/app');

  // 5. HTTPS server with hardened TLS configuration.
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // TLS configuration — Mozilla "Intermediate" profile (2024)
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // - TLS 1.2 + 1.3 only (TLS 1.0/1.1 disabled)
  // - Cipher suites ordered by preference
  // - X25519 + secp384r1 + secp256r1 (ECDHE curves)
  // - Server cipher preference (prevents BEAST attacks on TLS 1.0)
  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  const httpsOptions = {
    key: fs.readFileSync(config.tls.keyPath),
    cert: fs.readFileSync(config.tls.certPath),

    minVersion: 'TLSv1.2',
    maxVersion: 'TLSv1.3',
    honorCipherOrder: true,

    // Mozilla Intermediate cipher suites (TLS 1.2 + 1.3)
    // Node.js uses OpenSSL names, not IANA names.
    ciphers: [
      'TLS_AES_128_GCM_SHA256',           // TLS 1.3
      'TLS_AES_256_GCM_SHA384',           // TLS 1.3
      'TLS_CHACHA20_POLY1305_SHA256',     // TLS 1.3
      'ECDHE-ECDSA-AES128-GCM-SHA256',    // TLS 1.2
      'ECDHE-RSA-AES128-GCM-SHA256',      // TLS 1.2
      'ECDHE-ECDSA-AES256-GCM-SHA384',    // TLS 1.2
      'ECDHE-RSA-AES256-GCM-SHA384',      // TLS 1.2
      'ECDHE-ECDSA-CHACHA20-POLY1305',    // TLS 1.2
      'ECDHE-RSA-CHACHA20-POLY1305',      // TLS 1.2
    ].join(':'),

    // ECDHE curve preference (X25519 first — faster than P-256)
    ecdhCurve: 'X25519:secp384r1:secp256r1',
  };

  const server = https.createServer(httpsOptions, app);

  // Slowloris / DoS defenses
  server.requestTimeout = 10_000;
  server.headersTimeout = 12_000;
  server.keepAliveTimeout = 5_000;
  server.timeout = 15_000;

  server.listen(config.server.port, config.server.host, () => {
    logger.info(
      `HTTPS server running on https://${config.server.host}:${config.server.port} (env=${config.env})`
    );
  });

  // 6. Graceful shutdown.
  let shuttingDown = false;
  const shutdown = (signal) => {
    if (shuttingDown) return;
    shuttingDown = true;
    logger.info(`Received ${signal} — shutting down gracefully`);

    server.close((err) => {
      if (err) {
        logger.error('Error during server close', err);
        process.exit(1);
      }
      logger.info('Server closed cleanly');
      process.exit(0);
    });

    setTimeout(() => {
      logger.error('Forced exit after 5s shutdown timeout');
      process.exit(1);
    }, 5000).unref();
  };

  process.on('SIGINT', () => shutdown('SIGINT'));
  process.on('SIGTERM', () => shutdown('SIGTERM'));

  // 7. Last-resort error handlers.
  process.on('unhandledRejection', (reason) => {
    logger.error('Unhandled promise rejection', reason);
  });
  process.on('uncaughtException', (err) => {
    logger.error('Uncaught exception — shutting down', err);
    shutdown('uncaughtException');
  });
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Go
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bootstrap().catch((err) => {
  logger.error('Fatal startup error', err);
  process.exit(1);
});


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\src\app.js ===
// ============================================================================
// src/app.js — Express application assembly
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// This file wires the middleware chain and mounts routes.
// It does NOT start the server (that's server.js).
//
// Middleware order (critical):
//   1. Security headers
//   2. Body parsers (bounded)
//   3. Session cookies (signed)
//   4. Rate limiter
//   5. attachSession (DB-validated)
//   6. CSRF protection
//   7. Static files
//   8. Routes
//   9. 404 handler
//  10. Central error handler
// ============================================================================

const express = require('express');
const cookieSession = require('cookie-session');
const path = require('path');

const config = require('./config');
const logger = require('./utils/logger');
const { attachSession } = require('./middleware/auth');
const { csrfProtection } = require('./middleware/csrf');
const { generalLimiter } = require('./middleware/rateLimit');
const { notFound, errorHandler } = require('./middleware/errorHandler');
const routes = require('./routes');

const app = express();

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Trust proxy
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Enables correct client IP detection behind a reverse proxy (nginx, traefik).
// Required for rate limiting to work correctly in production.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
app.set('trust proxy', 1);

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Security headers
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Applied to every response. CSP is intentionally strict:
//   - default-src 'self'        → only same-origin resources by default
//   - script-src 'self'         → no inline scripts, no remote scripts
//   - style-src 'self' + inline → current stylesheet uses inline style attrs
//   - img-src 'self' + data:    → our media route + data URIs (icons)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
app.use((req, res, next) => {
  res.setHeader('X-Content-Type-Options', 'nosniff');
  res.setHeader('X-Frame-Options', 'DENY');
  res.setHeader('Referrer-Policy', 'strict-origin-when-cross-origin');
  res.setHeader(
    'Content-Security-Policy',
    "default-src 'self'; " +
    "script-src 'self'; " +
    "style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; " +
    "img-src 'self' data:; " +
    "form-action 'self'; " +
    "frame-ancestors 'none'; " +
    "base-uri 'self';"
  );
  res.setHeader('Permissions-Policy', 'geolocation=(), microphone=(), camera=()');
  
  // F04: Enhanced HSTS with preload and 2-year max-age
  res.setHeader(
    'Strict-Transport-Security',
    'max-age=63072000; includeSubDomains; preload'
  );
  
  next();
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Body parsers (bounded)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Global JSON limit (10kb default) prevents DoS via large payloads.
// Multipart uploads bypass these parsers and are handled by busboy
// directly in the media controller with their own limits.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
app.use(express.json({ limit: config.body.limit }));
app.use(express.urlencoded({ extended: false, limit: config.body.limit }));

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Sessions (signed cookie carrying only the session id)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// The cookie holds an opaque UUID, not user data.
// The actual session is stored server-side in the `sessions` table.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
app.use(
  cookieSession({
    name: config.session.name,
    keys: config.session.keys,
    maxAge: config.session.maxAgeMs,
    secure: config.session.secure,
    httpOnly: config.session.httpOnly,
    sameSite: config.session.sameSite,
  })
);

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Rate limiting
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Applied globally. Auth endpoints get a stricter limiter in their routes.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
app.use(generalLimiter);

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Attach user (DB-validated session)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Reads req.session.sid, looks it up in SQLite, and sets req.user.
// Never rejects — routes decide whether auth is required.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
app.use(attachSession);

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// CSRF protection
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// - Issues a signed token on every request (via cookie)
// - Verifies token on POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE via header, body, or query
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
app.use(csrfProtection);

// Expose the CSRF token to templates (if any server-rendered views use it)
app.use((req, res, next) => {
  res.locals.csrfToken = req.csrfToken;
  next();
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Static files
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Serves public/ (HTML, CSS, JS). Media files are NOT served here —
// they are streamed through /api/media/:id to enforce headers.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
app.use(express.static(path.join(__dirname, '..', 'public')));

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Application routes
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// All routes (F01, F02, F03, F04) are mounted via routes/index.js.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
app.use(routes);

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 404 handler
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Must come AFTER all routes. Returns JSON so API clients get a clean error.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
app.use(notFound);

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Central error handler
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Must be the LAST middleware. Never leaks internal details in production.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
app.use(errorHandler);

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Periodic cleanup tasks
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// These run in-process. In a multi-instance deployment, use a cron job
// or a leader election mechanism to avoid duplicate cleanup.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

// Expired sessions: hourly
setInterval(() => {
  try {
    const sessionModel = require('./models/session.model');
    sessionModel.deleteExpired();
  } catch (e) {
    logger.error('Session cleanup failed', e);
  }
}, 60 * 60 * 1000).unref();

// Expired OAuth states: every 10 minutes
setInterval(() => {
  try {
    const oauthStateModel = require('./models/oauthState.model');
    oauthStateModel.cleanupExpired();
  } catch (e) {
    logger.error('OAuth state cleanup failed', e);
  }
}, 10 * 60 * 1000).unref();

module.exports = app;


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\src\auth\identity.service.js ===
// ============================================================================
// src/auth/identity.service.js — Account linking policy
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// The security-critical heart of F02.
//
// Rules:
//   1. If (provider, provider_user_id) exists -> login to that user.
//   2. If email matches an existing user AND email is verified -> link.
//   3. If email matches an existing user BUT email is NOT verified -> REJECT.
//      Otherwise an attacker who registers a provider account with the
//      victim's unverified email could take over the victim's account.
//   4. If no match -> create a new user with an unusable password.
//
// "Link flow" (user already logged in and asks to attach a provider):
//   - If the identity exists and belongs to a different user -> reject.
//   - If it exists and belongs to the current user -> no-op success.
//   - If it doesn't exist -> attach it to the current user.
// ============================================================================

const crypto = require('crypto');

const db = require('../database/db');
const logger = require('../utils/logger');
const { HttpError } = require('../middleware/errorHandler');

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Prepared statements
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const stmtFindIdentity = db.prepare(`
  SELECT id, user_id FROM identities
  WHERE provider = ? AND provider_user_id = ?
`);

const stmtInsertIdentity = db.prepare(`
  INSERT INTO identities
    (user_id, provider, provider_user_id, email, display_name, avatar_url)
  VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
`);

const stmtFindUserByEmail = db.prepare(
  'SELECT id, email FROM users WHERE email = ?'
);

const stmtUsernameExists = db.prepare(
  'SELECT 1 FROM users WHERE username = ?'
);

const stmtInsertUser = db.prepare(
  'INSERT INTO users (username, email, password) VALUES (?, ?, ?)'
);

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Helpers
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

// SSO-only users get an unusable password. bcrypt always fails on it because
// it is not a valid bcrypt hash, so password login is effectively disabled.
function unusablePasswordHash() {
  return '!' + crypto.randomBytes(48).toString('base64url');
}

function buildUniqueUsername(base) {
  const clean =
    String(base || 'user')
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/[^a-z0-9_]/g, '_')
      .replace(/_+/g, '_')
      .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, '')
      .slice(0, 24) || 'user';

  let candidate = clean;
  let i = 1;
  while (stmtUsernameExists.get(candidate)) {
    candidate = `${clean}_${i++}`;
    if (candidate.length > 30) candidate = candidate.slice(0, 30);
  }
  return candidate;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Link flow (user already logged in)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const linkTx = db.transaction((linkUserId, profile) => {
  const existing = stmtFindIdentity.get(
    profile.provider,
    profile.providerUserId
  );

  if (existing) {
    if (existing.user_id !== linkUserId) {
      throw new HttpError(
        409,
        'This provider account is already linked to another user'
      );
    }
    return {
      userId: linkUserId,
      created: false,
      linked: false,
      alreadyLinked: true,
    };
  }

  stmtInsertIdentity.run(
    linkUserId,
    profile.provider,
    profile.providerUserId,
    profile.email || null,
    profile.displayName || null,
    profile.avatarUrl || null
  );

  logger.info(`Linked ${profile.provider} identity to user ${linkUserId}`);
  return { userId: linkUserId, created: false, linked: true, alreadyLinked: false };
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Login flow (user not logged in)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const loginTx = db.transaction((profile) => {
  const {
    provider,
    providerUserId,
    email,
    emailVerified,
    displayName,
    avatarUrl,
  } = profile;

  // Rule 1 — existing identity
  const existing = stmtFindIdentity.get(provider, providerUserId);
  if (existing) {
    return { userId: existing.user_id, created: false, linked: false };
  }

  // Rule 2 + 3 — existing user with matching email
  if (email) {
    const user = stmtFindUserByEmail.get(email);

    if (user) {
      if (!emailVerified) {
        throw new HttpError(
          409,
          'An account with this email already exists. Please sign in with your original method and link this provider from your profile.'
        );
      }

      stmtInsertIdentity.run(
        user.id,
        provider,
        providerUserId,
        email,
        displayName || null,
        avatarUrl || null
      );
      logger.info(`Linked ${provider} identity to existing user ${user.id}`);
      return { userId: user.id, created: false, linked: true };
    }
  }

  // Rule 4 — create new user
  if (!email) {
    throw new HttpError(
      400,
      'Provider did not return an email address; cannot create an account.'
    );
  }

  const username = buildUniqueUsername(displayName || email.split('@')[0]);
  const info = stmtInsertUser.run(username, email, unusablePasswordHash());
  const userId = info.lastInsertRowid;

  stmtInsertIdentity.run(
    userId,
    provider,
    providerUserId,
    email,
    displayName || null,
    avatarUrl || null
  );

  logger.info(`Created new user ${userId} via ${provider}`);
  return { userId, created: true, linked: false };
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Public API
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * Resolve a provider profile to a local user.
 * @param {object} profile  Provider profile (provider, providerUserId, email, ...)
 * @param {object} [opts]
 * @param {number|null} [opts.linkUserId]  If set, run the link flow for this user.
 */
function resolveUser(profile, opts = {}) {
  if (!profile || !profile.provider || !profile.providerUserId) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid provider profile');
  }

  if (opts.linkUserId) {
    return linkTx(opts.linkUserId, profile);
  }
  return loginTx(profile);
}

module.exports = { resolveUser, buildUniqueUsername };


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\src\auth\oidc.client.js ===
// ============================================================================
// src/auth/oidc.client.js — Google OIDC discovery (dynamic ESM import)
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// openid-client@6 is ESM-only. We lazy-load it with await import() inside
// the function, so this module is safe to require() from CommonJS.
//
// The client is cached (as a Promise) to avoid repeated discovery calls.
// ============================================================================

const config = require('../config');
const logger = require('../utils/logger');

// Cache: issuerUrl -> Promise<Client>
const cache = new Map();

async function getGoogleClient() {
  const key = config.sso.google.issuerUrl;
  if (cache.has(key)) return cache.get(key);

  const promise = (async () => {
    logger.info(`Discovering OIDC issuer: ${key}`);

    // Dynamic import: works with ESM-only packages from CommonJS.
    const { discovery, ClientSecretPost } = await import('openid-client');

    const issuerUrl = new URL(key);

    const client = await discovery(
      issuerUrl,
      config.sso.google.clientId,
      ClientSecretPost(config.sso.google.clientSecret)
    );

    return client;
  })();

  cache.set(key, promise);

  try {
    return await promise;
  } catch (err) {
    // Allow retry on next call if discovery failed.
    cache.delete(key);
    throw err;
  }
}

module.exports = { getGoogleClient };


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\src\auth\pkce.js ===
// ============================================================================
// src/auth/pkce.js — PKCE helpers (RFC 7636)
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// We keep our own implementation instead of relying on openid-client
// internals, so the module is portable and easy to test.
//
// All values are base64url-encoded without padding, as required by RFC 7636.
// ============================================================================

const crypto = require('crypto');

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// base64url encoding (RFC 4648 §5) without padding
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function base64url(buf) {
  return buf
    .toString('base64')
    .replace(/\+/g, '-')
    .replace(/\//g, '_')
    .replace(/=+$/, '');
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Code verifier
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 32 random bytes -> ~43 chars after base64url.
// RFC 7636 requires 43–128 characters from the unreserved set.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function generateCodeVerifier() {
  return base64url(crypto.randomBytes(32));
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Code challenge (S256)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// SHA-256 of the verifier, then base64url-encoded.
// The "plain" method is deliberately not implemented — it is insecure.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function generateCodeChallenge(verifier) {
  const digest = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(verifier).digest();
  return base64url(digest);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// State
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Random opaque value that binds the callback to this specific flow.
// Stored server-side, single-use, TTL-bounded.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function generateState() {
  return base64url(crypto.randomBytes(32));
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Nonce (OIDC)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Random value sent to the provider and verified inside the ID token.
// Prevents replay of an old ID token.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function generateNonce() {
  return base64url(crypto.randomBytes(16));
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Constant-time comparison
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Defense-in-depth: usable anywhere we compare two secrets.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function safeEqual(a, b) {
  if (typeof a !== 'string' || typeof b !== 'string') return false;
  const ab = Buffer.from(a);
  const bb = Buffer.from(b);
  if (ab.length !== bb.length) return false;
  return crypto.timingSafeEqual(ab, bb);
}

module.exports = {
  generateCodeVerifier,
  generateCodeChallenge,
  generateState,
  generateNonce,
  safeEqual,
};


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\src\auth\providers\github.provider.js ===
// ============================================================================
// src/auth/providers/github.provider.js — GitHub OAuth2 provider
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// GitHub does NOT support OIDC discovery, so we use raw OAuth2.
// We implement PKCE + state with our own helpers.
//
// GitHub email handling:
//   - /user may return null email (private profile).
//   - We fall back to /user/emails and pick the primary one.
// ============================================================================

const config = require('../../config');
const logger = require('../../utils/logger');

const TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000;

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// fetch with timeout and JSON parsing (with urlencoded fallback)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async function fetchJson(url, options = {}) {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), TIMEOUT_MS);

  try {
    const res = await fetch(url, {
      ...options,
      signal: controller.signal,
      headers: {
        Accept: 'application/json',
        'User-Agent': 'forum-app/1.0',
        ...(options.headers || {}),
      },
    });

    const text = await res.text();

    if (!res.ok) {
      logger.warn(`GitHub API error ${res.status}`, { url, text });
      throw new Error(`GitHub API error: ${res.status}`);
    }

    try {
      return JSON.parse(text);
    } catch {
      // GitHub sometimes returns urlencoded when Accept isn't honored.
      return Object.fromEntries(new URLSearchParams(text));
    }
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

function getRedirectUri(override) {
  return override || config.sso.github.redirectUri;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Step 1: Build the authorization URL
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function buildAuthUrl({ state, codeChallenge, redirectUri }) {
  const url = new URL(config.sso.github.authorizationEndpoint);

  url.searchParams.set('client_id', config.sso.github.clientId);
  url.searchParams.set('redirect_uri', getRedirectUri(redirectUri));
  url.searchParams.set('scope', config.sso.github.scope);
  url.searchParams.set('state', state);
  // GitHub supports PKCE since 2023.
  url.searchParams.set('code_challenge', codeChallenge);
  url.searchParams.set('code_challenge_method', 'S256');
  url.searchParams.set('allow_signup', 'true');

  return url.toString();
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Step 2: Exchange code for access token and fetch profile
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async function exchangeCode({ code, codeVerifier, redirectUri }) {
  const body = new URLSearchParams({
    client_id: config.sso.github.clientId,
    client_secret: config.sso.github.clientSecret,
    code,
    redirect_uri: getRedirectUri(redirectUri),
    code_verifier: codeVerifier,
  });

  const token = await fetchJson(config.sso.github.tokenEndpoint, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' },
    body: body.toString(),
  });

  if (!token.access_token) {
    throw new Error(
      `GitHub token exchange failed: ${token.error || 'unknown_error'}`
    );
  }

  const user = await fetchJson(config.sso.github.userInfoEndpoint, {
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token.access_token}` },
  });

  // GitHub may hide the primary email on /user. Fetch /user/emails.
  let email = user.email || null;
  let emailVerified = false;

  if (!email) {
    try {
      const emails = await fetchJson(config.sso.github.emailsEndpoint, {
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token.access_token}` },
      });
      const primary = emails.find((e) => e.primary) || emails[0];
      if (primary) {
        email = primary.email;
        emailVerified = primary.verified === true;
      }
    } catch (err) {
      logger.warn('Failed to fetch GitHub emails', { message: err.message });
    }
  }

  return {
    provider: 'github',
    providerUserId: String(user.id),
    email,
    emailVerified,
    displayName: user.name || user.login || 'GitHub User',
    avatarUrl: user.avatar_url || null,
  };
}

module.exports = { buildAuthUrl, exchangeCode };


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\src\auth\providers\google.provider.js ===
// ============================================================================
// src/auth/providers/google.provider.js — Google OIDC provider (dynamic ESM)
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// openid-client@6 is ESM-only. We lazy-load its named exports inside each
// function, so this module can be require()d from CommonJS without errors.
// ============================================================================

const config = require('../../config');

function getRedirectUri(override) {
  return override || config.sso.google.redirectUri;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Lazy loader for openid-client named exports.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async function loadOpenidClient() {
  return await import('openid-client');
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Step 1: Build the authorization URL
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async function buildAuthUrl({ state, nonce, codeChallenge, redirectUri }) {
  const { buildAuthorizationUrl } = await loadOpenidClient();
  const { getGoogleClient } = require('../oidc.client');
  const client = await getGoogleClient();

  const url = buildAuthorizationUrl(client, {
    redirect_uri: getRedirectUri(redirectUri),
    scope: 'openid email profile',
    state,
    nonce,
    code_challenge: codeChallenge,
    code_challenge_method: 'S256',
    prompt: 'select_account',
  });

  return url.toString();
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Step 2: Exchange the authorization code for tokens
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async function exchangeCode({
  code,
  state,
  codeVerifier,
  nonce,
  redirectUri,
  currentUrl,
}) {
  const { authorizationCodeGrant, fetchUserInfo } = await loadOpenidClient();
  const { getGoogleClient } = require('../oidc.client');
  const client = await getGoogleClient();

  // openid-client v6 expects the full callback URL as a URL object.
  const url =
    currentUrl instanceof URL
      ? currentUrl
      : new URL(
          currentUrl ||
            `${getRedirectUri(redirectUri)}?code=${encodeURIComponent(code)}&state=${encodeURIComponent(state)}`,
          config.sso.appBaseUrl
        );

  const tokens = await authorizationCodeGrant(client, url, {
    pkceCodeVerifier: codeVerifier,
    expectedState: state,
    expectedNonce: nonce,
    idTokenExpected: true,
  });

  const claims = tokens.claims();
  if (!claims) {
    throw new Error('Google returned no ID token claims');
  }

  // Optional: fetch userinfo for extra profile fields.
  let userinfo = null;
  try {
    userinfo = await fetchUserInfo(client, tokens.access_token, claims.sub);
  } catch (_) {
    // Non-fatal — we already have claims from the ID token.
  }

  return {
    provider: 'google',
    providerUserId: claims.sub,
    email: claims.email || (userinfo && userinfo.email) || null,
    emailVerified:
      claims.email_verified === true ||
      (userinfo && userinfo.email_verified === true) ||
      false,
    displayName:
      (userinfo && userinfo.name) ||
      claims.name ||
      claims.email ||
      'Google User',
    avatarUrl: (userinfo && userinfo.picture) || claims.picture || null,
  };
}

module.exports = { buildAuthUrl, exchangeCode };


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\src\auth\providers\index.js ===
// ============================================================================
// src/auth/providers/index.js — Provider registry
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Both providers expose the same interface:
//   - buildAuthUrl({ state, nonce, codeChallenge, redirectUri }) -> string
//   - exchangeCode({ code, state, codeVerifier, nonce, redirectUri, currentUrl })
//       -> { provider, providerUserId, email, emailVerified, displayName, avatarUrl }
//
// Note: buildAuthUrl is a naming convention we own. Google's underlying
// openid-client function is called `buildAuthorizationUrl`, but we alias it
// in google.provider.js to keep the two providers symmetrical.
// ============================================================================

const google = require('./google.provider');
const github = require('./github.provider');

const providers = {
  google: { name: 'google', ...google },
  github: { name: 'github', ...github },
};

function getProvider(name) {
  const p = providers[name];
  if (!p) {
    const err = new Error(`Unknown provider: ${name}`);
    err.status = 404;
    throw err;
  }
  return p;
}

function listEnabled() {
  // Lazy require to avoid circular dependency at module load time.
  const config = require('../../config');
  return {
    google: config.sso.google.enabled,
    github: config.sso.github.enabled,
  };
}

module.exports = { getProvider, providers, listEnabled };

// ============================================================================
// src/config/index.js — Environment loading and validation

require('dotenv').config();

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Helpers
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function required(name) {
  const value = process.env[name];
  if (!value || value.trim() === '') {
    throw new Error(`Missing required environment variable: ${name}`);
  }
  return value;
}

function optional(name, fallback) {
  const value = process.env[name];
  return value && value.trim() !== '' ? value : fallback;
}

function int(name, fallback) {
  const value = process.env[name];
  if (value === undefined || value === '') return fallback;
  const parsed = parseInt(value, 10);
  if (Number.isNaN(parsed)) {
    throw new Error(`Env var ${name} must be an integer`);
  }
  return parsed;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

const NODE_ENV = optional('NODE_ENV', 'development');
const isProd = NODE_ENV === 'production';

if (sessionSecret.length < 32) {
  throw new Error('SESSION_SECRET must be at least 32 characters long');

}

=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\src\controllers\auth.controller.js ===
// ============================================================================
// src/controllers/auth.controller.js — F04 hardened auth + F05 roles
// ============================================================================

const userModel = require('../models/user.model');
const sessionModel = require('../models/session.model');
const { hashPassword, verifyPassword } = require('../utils/password');
const { fingerprint } = require('../utils/hash');
const { HttpError } = require('../middleware/errorHandler');
const config = require('../config');

function uaHashFrom(req) {
  if (!config.session.bindToUserAgent) return null;
  const ua = req.get('user-agent');
  return ua ? fingerprint(ua) : null;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// POST /register
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async function register(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const { username, email, password } = req.body;

    if (userModel.findByEmail(email)) throw new HttpError(409, 'Email already registered');
    if (userModel.findByUsername(username)) throw new HttpError(409, 'Username taken');

    const hash = await hashPassword(password);
    
    // F05: First user becomes admin
    const role = userModel.countAll() === 0 ? 'admin' : 'user';
    const user = userModel.create(username, email, hash, role);

    res.status(201).json({ id: user.id, username: user.username, role: user.role });
  } catch (err) {
    if (err.code === 'SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE') {
      return next(new HttpError(409, 'Username or email already taken'));
    }
    next(err);
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// POST /login
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async function login(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const { username, password } = req.body;

    const user = userModel.findByUsername(username);
    if (!user) throw new HttpError(401, 'Invalid credentials');

    const ok = await verifyPassword(password, user.password);
    if (!ok) throw new HttpError(401, 'Invalid credentials');

    sessionModel.deleteAllForUser(user.id);

    const s = sessionModel.create(
      user.id,
      config.session.maxAgeMs,
      uaHashFrom(req)
    );
    req.session.sid = s.id;

    res.json({ id: user.id, username: user.username, role: user.role });
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// POST /logout
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function logout(req, res, next) {
  try {
    if (req.user && req.user.sessionId) {
      sessionModel.delete(req.user.sessionId);
    }
    req.session = null;
    res.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// GET /me
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function me(req, res) {
  if (!req.user) return res.status(401).json({ loggedIn: false });
  res.json({
    loggedIn: true,
    id: req.user.id,           // ⬅️ Added for F06 frontend ownership checks
    username: req.user.username,
    role: req.user.role,
    sessionId: req.user.sessionId,
  });
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// POST /api/users/me/password
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async function changePassword(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const userId = req.user.id;
    const { currentPassword, newPassword } = req.body;

    const user = userModel.findByIdWithPassword(userId);
    if (!user) throw new HttpError(404, 'User not found');

    const ok = await verifyPassword(currentPassword, user.password);
    if (!ok) throw new HttpError(401, 'Current password is incorrect');

    const newHash = await hashPassword(newPassword);
    const updated = userModel.updatePassword(userId, newHash);
    if (!updated) throw new HttpError(500, 'Failed to update password');

    const oldSessionId = req.user.sessionId;
    const newSession = sessionModel.rotate(
      oldSessionId,
      userId,
      config.session.maxAgeMs,
      uaHashFrom(req)
    );
    req.session.sid = newSession.id;

    res.json({
      ok: true,
      message: 'Password updated. Session rotated.',
    });
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
}

module.exports = { register, login, logout, me, changePassword };

// ============================================================================
// src/controllers/comments.controller.js — Comments + F06
// ============================================================================

const commentModel = require('../models/comment.model');
const postModel = require('../models/post.model');
const reactionModel = require('../models/reaction.model');
const notificationModel = require('../models/notification.model');
const activityModel = require('../models/activity.model');
const { HttpError } = require('../middleware/errorHandler');

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// GET /api/posts/:postId/comments
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function listComments(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const postId = parseInt(req.params.postId, 10);
    if (!Number.isInteger(postId) || postId <= 0) {
      throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid post id');
    }
    if (!postModel.findById(postId)) {
      throw new HttpError(404, 'Post not found');
    }
    res.json(commentModel.findByPost(postId));
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// POST /api/posts/:postId/comments
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function createComment(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const postId = parseInt(req.params.postId, 10);
    if (!Number.isInteger(postId) || postId <= 0) {
      throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid post id');
    }

    const post = postModel.findById(postId);
    if (!post) throw new HttpError(404, 'Post not found');

    const { content } = req.body;
    const c = commentModel.create(content.trim(), req.user.id, postId);

    // F06: Log activity
    activityModel.log(req.user.id, 'comment_create', 'comment', c.id);

    // F06: Notify post owner
    if (post.user_id !== req.user.id) {
      notificationModel.create(post.user_id, req.user.id, 'comment', 'post', postId);
    }

    res.status(201).json({ id: c.id });
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// PUT /api/comments/:commentId — F06 Edit (with optimistic locking)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function updateComment(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const commentId = parseInt(req.params.commentId, 10);
    if (!Number.isInteger(commentId) || commentId <= 0) {
      throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid comment id');
    }

    const comment = commentModel.findById(commentId);
    if (!comment) throw new HttpError(404, 'Comment not found');
    if (comment.user_id !== req.user.id) {
      throw new HttpError(403, 'You can only edit your own comments');
    }

    const { content, updatedAt } = req.body;

    const ok = commentModel.update(commentId, req.user.id, content.trim(), updatedAt);
    if (!ok) {
      throw new HttpError(409, 'Comment was modified by someone else. Please reload.');
    }

    // F06: Log activity

    res.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function deleteComment(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const commentId = parseInt(req.params.commentId, 10);
    if (!Number.isInteger(commentId) || commentId <= 0) {
      throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid comment id');
    }

    const comment = commentModel.findById(commentId);
    if (!comment) throw new HttpError(404, 'Comment not found');
    if (comment.user_id !== req.user.id && req.user.role !== 'admin') {
      throw new HttpError(403, 'You can only delete your own comments');
    }

    const ok = commentModel.delete(commentId, comment.user_id);
    if (!ok) throw new HttpError(500, 'Failed to delete comment');

    res.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function reactToComment(req, res, next) {

    const commentId = parseInt(req.params.commentId, 10);
    if (!Number.isInteger(commentId) || commentId <= 0) {
      throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid comment id');
    }

    const comment = commentModel.findById(commentId);
    if (!comment) throw new HttpError(404, 'Comment not found');

    const value = req.reactionValue;
    if (value !== 1 && value !== -1) {
      throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid reaction action');
    }

    const result = reactionModel.react(req.user.id, 'comment', commentId, value);

    // F06: Notify comment owner on like
    if (result.action === 'added' && value === 1 && comment.user_id !== req.user.id) {
      notificationModel.create(comment.user_id, req.user.id, 'like', 'comment', commentId);
    }

    res.json(result);
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
}

// ============================================================================
// src/controllers/media.controller.js — Media upload / serve / delete / list
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Upload flow:
//   1. Verify auth + post ownership + quota.
//   2. Run src/media/index.js::handleUpload(req) → full pipeline.
//   3. Insert DB row, respond with public URL.
//
// Serve flow:
//   - Public, streams file with server-computed MIME and nosniff header.
//   - Content-Disposition: inline with sanitized filename (numeric id).
//
// Delete flow:
//   - Owner only. Deletes DB row + file (ENOENT tolerated).
// ============================================================================

const fs = require('fs');

const { handleUpload } = require('../media');
const { resolveAbsolute, deleteFile } = require('../media/storage');
const mediaModel = require('../models/media.model');
const postModel = require('../models/post.model');
const config = require('../config');
const { HttpError } = require('../middleware/errorHandler');

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// POST /api/posts/:postId/media  (auth + owner of post)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async function uploadPostMedia(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const postId = parseInt(req.params.postId, 10);
    if (!Number.isInteger(postId) || postId <= 0) {
      throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid post id');
    }

    const post = postModel.findById(postId);
    if (!post) throw new HttpError(404, 'Post not found');
    if (post.user_id !== req.user.id) {
      throw new HttpError(403, 'You can only attach media to your own posts');
    }

    // Per-user quota check before consuming upload resources.
    const count = mediaModel.countForUser(req.user.id);
    if (count >= config.media.maxPerUser) {
      throw new HttpError(429, 'Media quota exceeded');
    }

    // Full pipeline: streaming → magic bytes → re-encode → store.
    const result = await handleUpload(req);

    const media = mediaModel.create({
      userId: req.user.id,
      postId,
      kind: 'post_image',
      originalName: result.originalName,
      mimeType: result.mime,
      sizeBytes: result.sizeBytes,
      width: result.width,
      height: result.height,
      storagePath: result.storagePath,
      sha256: result.sha256,
    });

    res.status(201).json({
      id: media.id,
      url: `/api/media/${media.id}`,
      width: media.width,
      height: media.height,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// GET /api/media/:id  (public read access)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function serveMedia(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const id = parseInt(req.params.id, 10);
    if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
      throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid media id');
    }

    const media = mediaModel.findById(id);
    if (!media) throw new HttpError(404, 'Media not found');

    const absolute = resolveAbsolute(media.storage_path);
    if (!fs.existsSync(absolute)) {
      throw new HttpError(404, 'Media file missing');
    }

    // Server-computed MIME; never trust the client.
    res.setHeader('Content-Type', media.mime_type);
    res.setHeader('Cache-Control', 'public, max-age=31536000, immutable');
    res.setHeader('X-Content-Type-Options', 'nosniff');
    res.setHeader(
      'Content-Disposition',
      `inline; filename="${media.id}.${media.mime_type.split('/')[1]}"`
    );

    fs.createReadStream(absolute).pipe(res);
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// DELETE /api/media/:id  (auth + owner)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async function deleteMedia(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const id = parseInt(req.params.id, 10);
    if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
      throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid media id');
    }

    const media = mediaModel.findById(id);
    if (!media) throw new HttpError(404, 'Media not found');
    if (media.user_id !== req.user.id) {
      throw new HttpError(403, 'You can only delete your own media');
    }

    // DB first, then file. If file deletion fails (ENOENT), it's tolerated.
    mediaModel.deleteById(id);
    await deleteFile(media.storage_path);

    res.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// GET /api/posts/:postId/media  (public list of a post's media)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function listPostMedia(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const postId = parseInt(req.params.postId, 10);
    if (!Number.isInteger(postId) || postId <= 0) {
      throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid post id');
    }

    const rows = mediaModel.listForPost(postId);

    res.json(
      rows.map((m) => ({
        id: m.id,
        url: `/api/media/${m.id}`,
        width: m.width,
        height: m.height,
      }))
    );
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
}

module.exports = {
  uploadPostMedia,
  serveMedia,
  deleteMedia,
  listPostMedia,
};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function changeUserRole(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const userId = parseInt(req.params.id, 10);
    if (!Number.isInteger(userId) || userId <= 0) throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid user id');

    const user = userModel.findById(userId);
    if (!user) throw new HttpError(404, 'User not found');

    // Admin cannot demote themselves to avoid locking out the only admin
    if (user.id === req.user.id && req.body.role !== 'admin') {
      throw new HttpError(400, 'You cannot demote yourself');
    }

    userModel.updateRole(userId, req.body.role);
    res.json({ ok: true, id: userId, role: req.body.role });
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
}

// ============================================================================
// src/controllers/posts.controller.js — Posts + F06 (edit, delete, notifications)
// ============================================================================

const postModel = require('../models/post.model');
const categoryModel = require('../models/category.model');
const reactionModel = require('../models/reaction.model');
const notificationModel = require('../models/notification.model');
const activityModel = require('../models/activity.model');
const { HttpError } = require('../middleware/errorHandler');
const config = require('../config');

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// GET /api/posts
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function listPosts(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const { category, filter, page, pageSize } = req.query;

    const limit = Math.min(
      parseInt(pageSize, 10) || config.pagination.defaultSize,
      config.pagination.maxSize
    );
    const pageNum = Math.max(parseInt(page, 10) || 1, 1);
    const offset = (pageNum - 1) * limit;

    const userId = req.user ? req.user.id : null;

    if ((filter === 'my_posts' || filter === 'my_likes') && !userId) {
      throw new HttpError(401, 'Login required for this filter');
    }

    if (category && !categoryModel.findByName(category)) {
      throw new HttpError(404, 'Category not found');
    }

    const { rows, total } = postModel.list({
      category,
      filter,
      userId,
      limit,
      offset,
    });

    res.json({
      data: rows.map((r) => ({
        ...r,
        categories: r.categories ? r.categories.split(',') : [],
      })),
      pagination: { page: pageNum, pageSize: limit, total },
    });
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// GET /api/posts/:postId
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function getPost(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const postId = parseInt(req.params.postId, 10);
    if (!Number.isInteger(postId) || postId <= 0) {
      throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid post id');
    }

    const post = postModel.findByIdAggregated(postId);
    if (!post) throw new HttpError(404, 'Post not found');

    const isOwner = req.user && req.user.id === post.user_id;
    const isMod = req.user && (req.user.role === 'moderator' || req.user.role === 'admin');

    // Security: Hide pending posts from unauthorized users by returning 404
    if (post.status !== 'approved' && !isOwner && !isMod) {
      throw new HttpError(404, 'Post not found');
    }

    res.json({
      ...post,
      categories: post.categories ? post.categories.split(',') : [],
    });
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// POST /api/posts
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function createPost(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const { title, content, categoryIds } = req.body;

    const allCategories = categoryModel.all();
    const knownIds = new Set(allCategories.map((c) => c.id));
    for (const id of categoryIds) {
      if (!knownIds.has(id)) {
        throw new HttpError(400, `Unknown category id: ${id}`);
      }
    }

    const postId = postModel.create(
      req.user.id,
      title.trim(),
      content.trim(),
      categoryIds
    );

    // F06: Log activity
    activityModel.log(req.user.id, 'post_create', 'post', postId);

    res.status(201).json({ id: postId });
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// PUT /api/posts/:postId — F06 Edit (with optimistic locking)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function updatePost(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const postId = parseInt(req.params.postId, 10);
    if (!Number.isInteger(postId) || postId <= 0) {
      throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid post id');
    }

    const post = postModel.findById(postId);
    if (!post) throw new HttpError(404, 'Post not found');
    if (post.user_id !== req.user.id) {
      throw new HttpError(403, 'You can only edit your own posts');
    }

    const { title, content, categoryIds, updatedAt } = req.body;

    const ok = postModel.update(
      postId,
      req.user.id,
      title.trim(),
      content.trim(),
      categoryIds,
      updatedAt
    );

    if (!ok) {
      throw new HttpError(409, 'Post was modified by someone else. Please reload.');
    }

    // F06: Log activity
    activityModel.log(req.user.id, 'post_edit', 'post', postId);

    res.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// DELETE /api/posts/:postId — F06 Delete
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function deletePost(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const postId = parseInt(req.params.postId, 10);
    if (!Number.isInteger(postId) || postId <= 0) {
      throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid post id');
    }

    const post = postModel.findById(postId);
    if (!post) throw new HttpError(404, 'Post not found');
    if (post.user_id !== req.user.id && req.user.role !== 'admin') {
      throw new HttpError(403, 'You can only delete your own posts');
    }

    const ok = postModel.delete(postId, post.user_id);
    if (!ok) throw new HttpError(500, 'Failed to delete post');

    res.json({ ok: true });
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// POST /api/posts/:postId/like | /dislike
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function reactToPost(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const postId = parseInt(req.params.postId, 10);
    if (!Number.isInteger(postId) || postId <= 0) {
      throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid post id');
    }

    const post = postModel.findById(postId);
    if (!post) throw new HttpError(404, 'Post not found');

    const value = req.reactionValue;
    if (value !== 1 && value !== -1) {
      throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid reaction action');
    }

    const result = reactionModel.react(req.user.id, 'post', postId, value);

    // F06: Notify post owner on like (not on dislike, not on self-like)
    if (result.action === 'added' && value === 1 && post.user_id !== req.user.id) {
      notificationModel.create(post.user_id, req.user.id, 'like', 'post', postId);
    }

    res.json(result);
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
}

=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\src\controllers\sso.controller.js ===
// ============================================================================
// src/controllers/sso.controller.js — SSO start / callback / status (F04)
// ============================================================================

const {
  generateCodeVerifier,
  generateCodeChallenge,
  generateState,
  generateNonce,
} = require('../auth/pkce');

const { getProvider, listEnabled } = require('../auth/providers');
const oauthStateModel = require('../models/oauthState.model');
const sessionModel = require('../models/session.model');
const identityService = require('../auth/identity.service');
const config = require('../config');
const logger = require('../utils/logger');
const { fingerprint } = require('../utils/hash');
const { HttpError } = require('../middleware/errorHandler');

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// sanitizeRedirect — same-origin only
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function sanitizeRedirect(to) {
  if (!to || typeof to !== 'string') return null;
  try {
    const base = new URL(config.sso.appBaseUrl);
    const target = new URL(to, base);
    if (target.origin !== base.origin) return null;
    return target.pathname + target.search;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// GET /auth/providers
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function status(req, res) {
  res.json({ providers: listEnabled() });
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// GET /auth/:provider — start flow
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async function start(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const providerName = req.params.provider;
    const provider = getProvider(providerName);

    const codeVerifier = generateCodeVerifier();
    const codeChallenge = generateCodeChallenge(codeVerifier);
    const state = generateState();
    const nonce = generateNonce();

    const linkUserId = req.user ? req.user.id : null;
    const redirectTo = sanitizeRedirect(req.query.redirect_to);

    oauthStateModel.create({
      state,
      codeVerifier,
      nonce,
      provider: providerName,
      redirectTo,
      linkUserId,
    });

    const url = await provider.buildAuthUrl({ state, nonce, codeChallenge });
    res.redirect(url);
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// GET /auth/:provider/callback — F04 hardened
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async function callback(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const providerName = req.params.provider;
    const provider = getProvider(providerName);

    const { code, state, error, error_description } = req.query;

    if (error) {
      throw new HttpError(400, `Provider error: ${error_description || error}`);
    }
    if (!code || !state) {
      throw new HttpError(400, 'Missing code or state');
    }

    // 1. Verify + consume state
    const stored = oauthStateModel.consume(state);
    if (!stored) throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid or expired state');
    if (stored.provider !== providerName) {
      throw new HttpError(400, 'Provider mismatch');
    }

    // 2. Exchange code
    const currentUrl = new URL(req.originalUrl, config.sso.appBaseUrl);
    const profile = await provider.exchangeCode({
      code,
      state,
      codeVerifier: stored.code_verifier,
      nonce: stored.nonce,
      currentUrl,
    });

    // 3. Resolve or link user
    const result = identityService.resolveUser(profile, {
      linkUserId: stored.link_user_id || null,
    });

    // 4. F04: global logout + create session with UA binding
    sessionModel.deleteAllForUser(result.userId);

    const ua = req.get('user-agent');
    const uaHash = (config.session.bindToUserAgent && ua)
      ? fingerprint(ua)
      : null;

    const session = sessionModel.create(
      result.userId,
      config.session.maxAgeMs,
      uaHash
    );
    req.session.sid = session.id;

    logger.info(
      `SSO callback ok: provider=${providerName} user=${result.userId} ` +
        `created=${result.created} linked=${result.linked}`
    );

    // 5. Redirect back
    const target = stored.redirect_to || '/';
    res.redirect(target);
  } catch (err) {
    next(err);
  }
}

module.exports = { status, start, callback };


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\src\database\db.js ===
// ============================================================================
// src/database/db.js — SQLite connection (singleton) with optional SQLCipher
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// P21: If config.db.password is set, the database is encrypted with SQLCipher.
// The password MUST be applied with PRAGMA key BEFORE any other operation.
// ============================================================================

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const Database = require('better-sqlite3-multiple-ciphers');

const config = require('../config');
const logger = require('../utils/logger');

const isMemory = config.db.path === ':memory:';

let db;

if (isMemory) {
  db = new Database(':memory:');
  logger.info('Database opened in-memory (:memory:)');
} else {
  const dbDir = path.dirname(config.db.path);
  if (!fs.existsSync(dbDir)) {
    fs.mkdirSync(dbDir, { recursive: true });
  }

  db = new Database(config.db.path);
  logger.info(`Database opened at ${config.db.path}`);
}

// --- P21: Apply encryption key (MUST be first) ---
if (config.db.password) {
  try {
    db.pragma(`key = '${config.db.password}'`);
    // Force a read to verify the key is correct
    db.prepare('SELECT count(*) AS c FROM sqlite_master').get();
    logger.info('Database encryption key applied (SQLCipher)');
  } catch (err) {
    throw new Error(
      'Failed to open encrypted database. ' +
        'Check DB_PASSWORD (or the DB was created without encryption). ' +
        err.message
    );
  }
}

// Foreign keys are OFF by default in SQLite. We need them for CASCADE.
db.pragma('foreign_keys = ON');

// WAL mode (works with SQLCipher too)
if (!isMemory) {
  db.pragma('journal_mode = WAL');
}

module.exports = db;

// ============================================================================
// src/database/schema.js — Migration runner
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Every schema change is a numbered migration. The `schema_migrations` table
// records which migrations have been applied. Running this module on every
// boot is safe: applied migrations are skipped.
//
// Migration rules:
//   - Never modify an existing migration.
//   - Add new migrations with the next number (011_, 012_, ...).
//   - Each migration runs inside a transaction (SQLite DDL is transactional).
// ============================================================================

const db = require('./db');
const logger = require('../utils/logger');

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Bootstrap: migrations table
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Must exist before we can check which migrations have been applied.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
db.exec(`
  CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schema_migrations (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    name TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    applied_at DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
  )
`);

];

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Runner
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function runMigrations() {
  const applied = new Set(
    db.prepare('SELECT name FROM schema_migrations').all().map((r) => r.name)
  );

  for (const m of migrations) {
    if (applied.has(m.name)) continue;

    const tx = db.transaction(() => {
      m.up();
      db.prepare('INSERT INTO schema_migrations (name) VALUES (?)').run(m.name);
    });

    try {
      tx();
      logger.info(`Migration applied: ${m.name}`);
    } catch (err) {
      logger.error(`Migration failed: ${m.name}`, err);
      throw err;
    }
  }
}

module.exports = { runMigrations };

// ============================================================================
// src/database/seed.js — Default data seeding (idempotent)
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Inserts default categories. Safe to run on every boot:
// INSERT OR IGNORE skips rows that already exist (UNIQUE constraint on name).
//
// Can be run as a script: `npm run db:seed`
// Or imported: `const { seed } = require('./seed'); seed();`
// ============================================================================

const db = require('./db');
const { runMigrations } = require('./schema');
const logger = require('../utils/logger');

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Default categories
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// These are the "subforums" that ship with a fresh install.
// Admins can add more later; these are just to make the forum usable
// immediately after installation.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const DEFAULT_CATEGORIES = [
  'General',
  'Technology',
  'Sports',
  'Science',
];

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Seed function
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function seed() {
  // Ensure the schema is up to date before inserting.
  runMigrations();

  const stmt = db.prepare('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO categories (name) VALUES (?)');

  const tx = db.transaction((names) => {
    for (const n of names) stmt.run(n);
  });

  tx(DEFAULT_CATEGORIES);

  logger.info(`Seeded ${DEFAULT_CATEGORIES.length} categories`);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// CLI entry point
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// When run directly (`node src/database/seed.js`), execute and exit.
// When required as a module, expose the function only.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
if (require.main === module) {
  try {
    seed();
    // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
    console.log('Seed complete.');
    process.exit(0);
  } catch (err) {
    // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
    console.error('Seed failed:', err);
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

module.exports = { seed };
