// ============================================================================
// src/media/detect.js — Magic-byte detection via file-type@16 (CJS)
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// file-type@16 exports an object with a `fromBuffer()` method.
// (v17+ renamed it to `fileTypeFromBuffer`; v18+ is ESM-only.)
//
// We NEVER trust the client's Content-Type header. Only magic bytes at the
// start of the buffer decide the type.
//
// SVG is deliberately excluded (XSS vector via inline <script>).
// ============================================================================

const FileType = require('file-type');
const { HttpError } = require('../middleware/errorHandler');

// Allow-list. Anything not in this set is rejected.
const ALLOWED = new Set([
  'image/jpeg',
  'image/png',
  'image/webp',
  'image/gif',
]);

async function detectImageType(buffer) {
  // file-type@16 uses `fromBuffer`, not `fileTypeFromBuffer`.
  const type = await FileType.fromBuffer(buffer);

  if (!type || !ALLOWED.has(type.mime)) {
    throw new HttpError(
      415,
      'Unsupported file type. Allowed: JPEG, PNG, WebP, GIF.'
    );
  }

  return { ext: type.ext, mime: type.mime };
}

module.exports = { detectImageType };


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\src\media\index.js ===
// ============================================================================
// src/media/index.js — Unified media pipeline entry point
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Runs all four stages in order:
//   1. Streaming intake (busboy)
//   2. Magic-byte detection (file-type)
//   3. Decode + re-encode (sharp)
//   4. Atomic storage (SHA-256)
//
// Returns a plain object suitable for inserting into the media table.
// ============================================================================

const { intakeMultipart } = require('./upload');
const { detectImageType } = require('./detect');
const { processImage } = require('./process');
const { storeBuffer } = require('./storage');

async function handleUpload(req) {
  // 1. Stream + bound
  const { buffer, originalName, declaredMime, fields } =
    await intakeMultipart(req);

  // 2. Magic bytes (rejects non-image, SVG, etc.)
  await detectImageType(buffer);

  // 3. Decode + re-encode (strips EXIF, destroys polyglots)
  const processed = await processImage(buffer);

  // 4. Store (atomic, SHA-256-addressed)
  const stored = await storeBuffer(processed.buffer, processed.ext);

  return {
    originalName,
    declaredMime,
    fields,
    mime: processed.mime,
    width: processed.width,
    height: processed.height,
    sizeBytes: processed.sizeBytes,
    storagePath: stored.relativePath,
    sha256: stored.sha256,
  };
}

module.exports = { handleUpload };


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\src\media\process.js ===
// ============================================================================
// src/media/process.js — Decode + re-encode with sharp
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// This is the security-critical step of the media pipeline.
//
// Why re-encode?
//   sharp decodes the image to raw pixel data and re-encodes it into a
//   brand-new file. Any appended bytes, polyglot payloads, or malicious EXIF
//   data are destroyed in the process.
//
// Order of operations:
//   1. Read metadata (dimensions) BEFORE full decode → decompression bomb guard
//   2. Reject if dimensions exceed config.media.max{Width,Height}
//   3. sharp.rotate() → applies EXIF rotation, then strips EXIF
//   4. resize() with `withoutEnlargement: true` → downscale only
//   5. Encode to WebP (or JPEG) at configurable quality
// ============================================================================

const sharp = require('sharp');

const config = require('../config');
const { HttpError } = require('../middleware/errorHandler');

async function processImage(buffer) {
  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // 1. Read metadata (no full decode yet)
  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  let meta;
  try {
    meta = await sharp(buffer, {
      failOn: 'error',
      limitInputPixels: false,
    }).metadata();
  } catch (err) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid or corrupted image');
  }

  if (!meta || !meta.width || !meta.height) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Image has no readable dimensions');
  }

  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // 2. Dimension guard (before decoding pixels)
  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  if (
    meta.width > config.media.maxWidth ||
    meta.height > config.media.maxHeight
  ) {
    throw new HttpError(
      413,
      `Image too large. Max ${config.media.maxWidth}x${config.media.maxHeight}.`
    );
  }

  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // 3. Build the pipeline
  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  const pipeline = sharp(buffer, { failOn: 'error' })
    .rotate()
    .resize({
      width: config.media.resizeMaxWidth,
      height: config.media.resizeMaxHeight,
      fit: 'inside',
      withoutEnlargement: true,
    });

  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // 4. Encode
  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  let outputBuffer;
  let outputMime;

  if (config.media.outputFormat === 'jpeg') {
    outputBuffer = await pipeline
      .jpeg({ quality: config.media.outputQuality, mozjpeg: true })
      .toBuffer();
    outputMime = 'image/jpeg';
  } else {
    outputBuffer = await pipeline
      .webp({ quality: config.media.outputQuality })
      .toBuffer();
    outputMime = 'image/webp';
  }

  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // 5. Read final dimensions
  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  const finalMeta = await sharp(outputBuffer).metadata();

  return {
    buffer: outputBuffer,
    mime: outputMime,
    ext: config.media.outputFormat === 'jpeg' ? 'jpg' : 'webp',
    width: finalMeta.width,
    height: finalMeta.height,
    sizeBytes: outputBuffer.length,
  };
}

module.exports = { processImage };


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\src\media\storage.js ===
// ============================================================================
// src/media/storage.js — Atomic storage with SHA-256 addressing
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Storage path format: <root>/<ab>/<cd>/<sha256>.<ext>
//
//   - `ab` and `cd` are the first 4 hex chars of the hash (directory sharding).
//   - The final filename is the full SHA-256 in hex.
//   - The extension comes from the re-encoded output, NOT from user input.
//
// Properties:
//   - Deduplication: identical bytes always produce the same path.
//   - Atomic write: temp file + rename.
//   - Idempotent: if the file already exists, we reuse it.
//   - Path traversal impossible: paths are hex-only + resolveAbsolute guard.
// ============================================================================

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const crypto = require('crypto');

const config = require('../config');
const logger = require('../utils/logger');

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// SHA-256 of a buffer
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function sha256(buffer) {
  return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(buffer).digest('hex');
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Build the relative path from a hash and extension
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function buildRelativePath(hash, ext) {
  const ab = hash.slice(0, 2);
  const cd = hash.slice(2, 4);
  return path.join(ab, cd, `${hash}.${ext}`);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Ensure a directory exists (recursive)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function ensureDir(dir) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) {
    fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Store a buffer, returning the relative path and hash
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async function storeBuffer(buffer, ext) {
  const hash = sha256(buffer);
  const relative = buildRelativePath(hash, ext);
  const absolute = path.join(config.media.root, relative);

  ensureDir(path.dirname(absolute));

  // Idempotent: if same bytes already stored, reuse.
  if (fs.existsSync(absolute)) {
    return { relativePath: relative, absolutePath: absolute, sha256: hash };
  }

  // Atomic write: write to a temp file, then rename.
  const tmp = `${absolute}.tmp.${process.pid}.${Date.now()}`;
  await fs.promises.writeFile(tmp, buffer, { flag: 'wx' });
  await fs.promises.rename(tmp, absolute);

  logger.info(`Stored media: ${relative} (${buffer.length} bytes)`);

  return { relativePath: relative, absolutePath: absolute, sha256: hash };
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Resolve a relative path to an absolute one, guarding against traversal
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function resolveAbsolute(relativePath) {
  const absolute = path.resolve(config.media.root, relativePath);
  const root = path.resolve(config.media.root);

  if (!absolute.startsWith(root + path.sep)) {
    throw new Error('Refusing to resolve path outside media root');
  }

  return absolute;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Delete a stored file (ENOENT tolerated)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async function deleteFile(relativePath) {
  const absolute = resolveAbsolute(relativePath);
  try {
    await fs.promises.unlink(absolute);
  } catch (err) {
    if (err.code !== 'ENOENT') throw err;
  }
}

module.exports = { storeBuffer, resolveAbsolute, deleteFile, sha256 };


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\src\media\upload.js ===
// ============================================================================
// src/media/upload.js — Streaming multipart intake (busboy)
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Hard bounds enforced during streaming:
//   - Total bytes (config.media.maxFileBytes, default 5 MB)
//   - Number of files (1)
//   - Number of fields (5)
//   - Number of parts (10)
//   - Field name size (100 bytes)
//   - Field value size (1024 bytes)
//
// The stream is DRAINED (not aborted) when a limit is exceeded, to avoid
// connection resets that could be exploited.
//
// Returns a Promise that resolves with { buffer, originalName, declaredMime, fields }
// or rejects with an HttpError.
// ============================================================================

const Busboy = require('busboy');

const config = require('../config');
const { HttpError } = require('../middleware/errorHandler');

function intakeMultipart(req) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    let bb;

    try {
      bb = Busboy({
        headers: req.headers,
        limits: {
          fileSize: config.media.maxFileBytes,
          files: 1,
          fields: 5,
          parts: 10,
          fieldNameSize: 100,
          fieldSize: 1024,
        },
      });
    } catch (err) {
      return reject(new HttpError(400, 'Invalid multipart request'));
    }

    const fields = {};
    const chunks = [];
    let totalBytes = 0;
    let fileBuffer = null;
    let originalName = null;
    let declaredMime = null;
    let truncated = false;
    let fileSeen = false;

    bb.on('field', (name, value) => {
      fields[name] = value;
    });

    bb.on('file', (name, stream, info) => {
      if (fileSeen) {
        stream.resume();
        return reject(new HttpError(400, 'Only one file is allowed'));
      }

      fileSeen = true;
      originalName = info.filename || 'unnamed';
      declaredMime = info.mimeType || 'application/octet-stream';

      stream.on('data', (chunk) => {
        totalBytes += chunk.length;
        if (totalBytes > config.media.maxFileBytes) {
          truncated = true;
          stream.resume(); // drain
          return;
        }
        chunks.push(chunk);
      });

      stream.on('limit', () => {
        truncated = true;
      });

      stream.on('end', () => {
        if (truncated) return;
        fileBuffer = Buffer.concat(chunks, totalBytes);
      });

      stream.on('error', (err) =>
        reject(new HttpError(400, `Upload stream error: ${err.message}`))
      );
    });

    bb.on('filesLimit', () => reject(new HttpError(400, 'Too many files')));
    bb.on('fieldsLimit', () => reject(new HttpError(400, 'Too many fields')));
    bb.on('partsLimit', () => reject(new HttpError(400, 'Too many parts')));

    bb.on('error', (err) =>
      reject(new HttpError(400, `Multipart error: ${err.message}`))
    );

    bb.on('close', () => {
      if (truncated) return reject(new HttpError(413, 'File too large'));
      if (!fileBuffer) return reject(new HttpError(400, 'No file provided'));

      resolve({
        buffer: fileBuffer,
        originalName,
        declaredMime,
        fields,
      });
    });

    req.pipe(bb);
  });
}

module.exports = { intakeMultipart };

// ============================================================================
// src/middleware/auth.js — Session attachment and auth guard
// ============================================================================

const sessionModel = require('../models/session.model');
const { fingerprint } = require('../utils/hash');
const { HttpError } = require('./errorHandler');

function attachSession(req, res, next) {
  req.user = null;

  const sid = req.session && req.session.sid;
  if (sid) {
    const uaHash = req.get('user-agent') ? fingerprint(req.get('user-agent')) : null;
    const row = sessionModel.findValid(sid, uaHash);

    if (row) {
      req.user = {
        id: row.user_id,
        username: row.username,
        sessionId: row.id,
        role: row.role, // ⬅️ F05: Role from session JOIN
      };
    } else {
      req.session = null;
    }
  }

  next();
}

function requireAuth(req, res, next) {
  if (!req.user) return next(new HttpError(401, 'Authentication required'));
  next();
}

function requireModerator(req, res, next) {
  if (!req.user) return next(new HttpError(401, 'Authentication required'));
  if (req.user.role !== 'moderator' && req.user.role !== 'admin') {
    return next(new HttpError(403, 'Moderator or Admin access required'));
  }
  next();
}

function requireAdmin(req, res, next) {
  if (!req.user) return next(new HttpError(401, 'Authentication required'));
  if (req.user.role !== 'admin') {
    return next(new HttpError(403, 'Admin access required'));
  }
  next();
}

module.exports = { attachSession, requireAuth, requireModerator, requireAdmin };


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\src\middleware\csrf.js ===
// ============================================================================
// src/middleware/csrf.js — Double-submit cookie CSRF protection
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// cookie-session does NOT expose req.cookies. We read the CSRF cookie
// directly from req.headers.cookie with a tiny parser (no cookie-parser dep).
//
// Token format: <raw>.<signature>
//   signature = HMAC-SHA256(raw, CSRF_SECRET)
// ============================================================================

const crypto = require('crypto');

const config = require('../config');
const { HttpError } = require('./errorHandler');

const SAFE_METHODS = new Set(['GET', 'HEAD', 'OPTIONS']);

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// readCookie — parse a single cookie from req.headers.cookie
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Handles URL-encoded values and cookies with '=' inside the value.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function readCookie(req, name) {
  const header = req.headers.cookie;
  if (!header) return null;

  for (const part of header.split(';')) {
    const eq = part.indexOf('=');
    if (eq === -1) continue;

    const k = part.slice(0, eq).trim();
    if (k !== name) continue;

    const v = part.slice(eq + 1).trim();
    try {
      return decodeURIComponent(v);
    } catch {
      return v;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// HMAC signature
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function sign(token) {
  return crypto
    .createHmac('sha256', config.csrf.secret)
    .update(token)
    .digest('hex');
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// issueToken — set a signed csrf_token cookie and return its value
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function issueToken(res) {
  const raw = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
  const signed = `${raw}.${sign(raw)}`;

  res.cookie(config.csrf.cookieName, signed, {
    httpOnly: false, // must be readable by frontend JS
    secure: config.session.secure,
    sameSite: 'lax',
    maxAge: config.session.maxAgeMs,
    path: '/',
  });

  return signed;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// verify — constant-time comparison of HMACs
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function verify(raw, signed) {
  if (!raw || !signed) return false;
  if (!signed.startsWith(raw + '.')) return false;

  const expected = sign(raw);
  const provided = signed.slice(raw.length + 1);

  const a = Buffer.from(expected, 'hex');
  const b = Buffer.from(provided, 'hex');

  if (a.length !== b.length) return false;
  return crypto.timingSafeEqual(a, b);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// csrfProtection
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function csrfProtection(req, res, next) {
  // Read the token from the cookie (manually parsed).
  let token = readCookie(req, config.csrf.cookieName);

  // Issue a fresh token if none exists (safe methods + first visit).
  if (!token) token = issueToken(res);

  req.csrfToken = token;

  // Safe methods: no verification needed.
  if (SAFE_METHODS.has(req.method)) return next();

  // Unsafe methods: require a matching token from header, body, or query.
  const fromHeader = req.get(config.csrf.headerName);
  const fromBody = req.body && req.body[config.csrf.fieldName];
  const fromQuery = req.query && req.query[config.csrf.fieldName];
  const provided = fromHeader || fromBody || fromQuery;

  if (!provided) {
    return next(new HttpError(403, 'Missing CSRF token'));
  }

  const [raw] = String(provided).split('.');
  const cookieRaw = String(token).split('.')[0];

  if (cookieRaw !== raw) {
    return next(new HttpError(403, 'CSRF token mismatch'));
  }
  if (!verify(raw, String(provided))) {
    return next(new HttpError(403, 'Invalid CSRF token'));
  }

  next();
}

module.exports = { csrfProtection, issueToken };


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\src\middleware\errorHandler.js ===
// ============================================================================
// src/middleware/errorHandler.js — HttpError + 404 + Central error handler
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// In production, 5xx responses never leak internal details. Full stack is
// logged server-side only.
// ============================================================================

const logger = require('../utils/logger');
const config = require('../config');

class HttpError extends Error {
  constructor(status, message, code) {
    super(message);
    this.status = status;
    this.code = code;
  }
}

// 404 for unknown routes (JSON, not HTML — API-friendly)
function notFound(req, res) {
  res.status(404).json({ error: 'Not found' });
}

// Central error handler — must be the last middleware.
// eslint-disable-next-line no-unused-vars
function errorHandler(err, req, res, next) {
  const status = err.status || 500;

  if (status >= 500) {
    logger.error('Unhandled error', {
      message: err.message,
      stack: err.stack,
      path: req.originalUrl,
      method: req.method,
    });
  }

  const safeMessage =
    status >= 500
      ? 'Internal server error'
      : err.message || 'Request error';

  res.status(status).json({
    error: safeMessage,
    // In development, include code + stack for debugging.
    ...(config.isProd ? {} : { code: err.code, stack: err.stack }),
  });
}

module.exports = { HttpError, notFound, errorHandler };

// ============================================================================
// src/middleware/mediaUpload.js — Content-Type guard for upload routes
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Ensures the request is multipart/form-data before we reach the controller.
// Actual parsing is done by busboy inside src/media/upload.js.
//
// We don't use express.json() here — multipart bodies bypass it.
// ============================================================================

function mediaUploadGuard(req, res, next) {
  const ct = req.get('content-type') || '';
  if (!ct.startsWith('multipart/form-data')) {
    return res.status(415).json({ error: 'Expected multipart/form-data' });
  }
  next();
}

module.exports = { mediaUploadGuard };


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\src\middleware\rateLimit.js ===
// ============================================================================
// src/middleware/rateLimit.js — F04 rate limiters (IPv6-safe)
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// All custom keyGenerators that fall back to IP must use ipKeyGenerator()
// to apply IPv6 subnet masking (/56) and prevent per-address bypass.
// ============================================================================

const rateLimit = require('express-rate-limit');
const { ipKeyGenerator } = require('express-rate-limit');
const config = require('../config');

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Key generators
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// userOrIpKey — for authenticated routes (req.user is guaranteed)
function userOrIpKey(req) {
  if (req.user && req.user.id) return `u:${req.user.id}`;
  // IPv6-safe fallback
  return `ip:${ipKeyGenerator(req.ip)}`;
}

// usernameOrIpKey — for login (prevents brute-force across IPs)
function usernameOrIpKey(req) {
  const u = (req.body && req.body.username) || '';
  if (u) return `login:${String(u).toLowerCase()}`;
  // IPv6-safe fallback
  return `ip:${ipKeyGenerator(req.ip)}`;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Global flood defense (IP-based, applied before auth)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const generalLimiter = rateLimit({
  windowMs: config.rateLimit.windowMs,
  max: config.rateLimit.max,
  standardHeaders: true,
  legacyHeaders: false,
  message: { error: 'Too many requests, please slow down.' },
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Auth flood defense (IP-based, applied on register)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const authLimiter = rateLimit({
  windowMs: config.rateLimit.windowMs,
  max: config.rateLimit.authMax,
  standardHeaders: true,
  legacyHeaders: false,
  message: { error: 'Too many authentication attempts.' },
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Login: username-based (targeted brute-force defense)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const loginLimiter = rateLimit({
  windowMs: 15 * 60 * 1000,
  max: 10,
  standardHeaders: true,
  legacyHeaders: false,
  keyGenerator: usernameOrIpKey,
  message: { error: 'Too many login attempts for this account.' },
  skipSuccessfulRequests: false,
  skipFailedRequests: false,
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Post creation: user-based
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const postCreateLimiter = rateLimit({
  windowMs: 5 * 60 * 1000,
  max: 10,
  standardHeaders: true,
  legacyHeaders: false,
  keyGenerator: userOrIpKey,
  message: { error: 'Too many posts created. Please wait.' },
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Comment creation: user-based
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const commentCreateLimiter = rateLimit({
  windowMs: 5 * 60 * 1000,
  max: 20,
  standardHeaders: true,
  legacyHeaders: false,
  keyGenerator: userOrIpKey,
  message: { error: 'Too many comments. Please slow down.' },
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Reactions (like/dislike): user-based
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const reactionLimiter = rateLimit({
  windowMs: 60 * 1000,
  max: 60,
  standardHeaders: true,
  legacyHeaders: false,
  keyGenerator: userOrIpKey,
  message: { error: 'Too many reactions. Please slow down.' },
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Media upload: user-based
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const uploadLimiter = rateLimit({
  windowMs: 60 * 1000,
  max: 10,
  standardHeaders: true,
  legacyHeaders: false,
  keyGenerator: userOrIpKey,
  message: { error: 'Too many uploads, please wait.' },
});

module.exports = {
  generalLimiter,
  authLimiter,
  loginLimiter,
  postCreateLimiter,
  commentCreateLimiter,
  reactionLimiter,
  uploadLimiter,
};

// ============================================================================
// src/middleware/validate.js — Validator wrapper
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Wraps a synchronous validator function so it can be used as Express
// middleware. The validator throws HttpError on failure.
// ============================================================================

const { HttpError } = require('./errorHandler');

function validate(validatorFn) {
  return (req, res, next) => {
    try {
      validatorFn(req);
      next();
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof HttpError) return next(err);
      next(new HttpError(400, err.message || 'Invalid request'));
    }
  };
}

module.exports = { validate };

const db = require('../database/db');

const stmtInsert = db.prepare(
  'INSERT INTO comments (content, user_id, post_id, status, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, strftime(\'%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%f\', \'now\'))'
);

const stmtByPost = db.prepare(`
  SELECT c.id, c.content, c.created_at, c.updated_at, c.status, c.user_id, u.username AS author,
    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN r.value = 1  THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) AS likes_count,
    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN r.value = -1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) AS dislikes_count
  FROM comments c JOIN users u ON u.id = c.user_id
  LEFT JOIN reactions r ON r.target_type = 'comment' AND r.target_id = c.id
  WHERE c.post_id = ? AND c.status = 'approved'
  GROUP BY c.id ORDER BY c.created_at ASC
`);

const stmtById = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM comments WHERE id = ?');
const stmtUpdateStatus = db.prepare('UPDATE comments SET status = ? WHERE id = ?');

const stmtUpdateContent = db.prepare(`
  UPDATE comments 
  SET content = ?, updated_at = strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%f', 'now')
  WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ? AND updated_at = ?
`);

const stmtDelete = db.prepare('DELETE FROM comments WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?');

function listPending() {
  return db.prepare(`
    SELECT c.id, c.content, c.created_at, c.status, u.username AS author, p.title AS post_title, c.post_id
    FROM comments c JOIN users u ON u.id = c.user_id JOIN posts p ON p.id = c.post_id
    WHERE c.status = 'pending' ORDER BY c.created_at ASC
  `).all();
}

module.exports = {
  findByPost: (postId) => stmtByPost.all(postId),
  findById: (id) => stmtById.get(id),
  create: (content, userId, postId, status = 'approved') => {
    const info = stmtInsert.run(content, userId, postId, status);
    return { id: info.lastInsertRowid };
  },
  updateStatus: (id, status) => stmtUpdateStatus.run(status, id).changes > 0,
  update: (commentId, userId, content, expectedUpdatedAt) => {
    const info = stmtUpdateContent.run(content, commentId, userId, expectedUpdatedAt);
    return info.changes > 0;
  },
  delete: (commentId, userId) => {
    const info = stmtDelete.run(commentId, userId);
    return info.changes > 0;
  },
  listPending,
};

=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\src\models\oauthState.model.js ===
// ============================================================================
// src/models/oauthState.model.js — Single-use OAuth state store
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Stores state, PKCE verifier, and nonce server-side.
// consume() is atomic (transaction: SELECT + DELETE) and single-use.
//
// expires_at format: SQLite 'YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS' (UTC) — same as
// CURRENT_TIMESTAMP. All comparisons wrap both sides in datetime() so that
// any legacy rows written in ISO 8601 ('2026-01-01T12:10:00.000Z') are still
// evaluated correctly instead of being treated as "never expires".
// ============================================================================

const db = require('../database/db');
const config = require('../config');

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Timestamp helper — ISO → SQLite format (UTC)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function toSqliteTimestamp(date) {
  return date.toISOString().replace('T', ' ').slice(0, 19);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Prepared statements
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const stmtInsert = db.prepare(`
  INSERT INTO oauth_states
    (state, code_verifier, nonce, provider, redirect_to, link_user_id, expires_at)
  VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
`);

// datetime() normalizes both sides so legacy ISO rows and SQLite-format rows
// are compared on equal footing. Without it, a text comparison between
// '2026-01-01T...' and '2026-01-01 ...' would ALWAYS be true (T > space).
const stmtTake = db.prepare(`
  SELECT * FROM oauth_states
  WHERE state = ? AND datetime(expires_at) > datetime('now')
`);

const stmtDelete = db.prepare('DELETE FROM oauth_states WHERE state = ?');

const stmtCleanup = db.prepare(`
  DELETE FROM oauth_states WHERE datetime(expires_at) <= datetime('now')
`);

// Atomic single-use: read + delete inside a transaction.
const consumeTx = db.transaction((state) => {
  const row = stmtTake.get(state);
  if (!row) return null;
  stmtDelete.run(state);
  return row;
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Public API
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
module.exports = {
  create: ({ state, codeVerifier, nonce, provider, redirectTo, linkUserId }) => {
    const expiresAt = toSqliteTimestamp(
      new Date(Date.now() + config.sso.stateTtlMs)
    );
    stmtInsert.run(
      state,
      codeVerifier,
      nonce,
      provider,
      redirectTo || null,
      linkUserId || null,
      expiresAt
    );
  },
  consume: (state) => consumeTx(state),
  cleanupExpired: () => stmtCleanup.run(),
};

// src/models/post.model.js — Posts with filters, aggregates, moderation, F06

const db = require('../database/db');
const config = require('../config');

const stmtInsert = db.prepare(
  'INSERT INTO posts (title, content, user_id, status, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, strftime(\'%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%f\', \'now\'))'
);

const stmtAttachCategory = db.prepare(
  'INSERT OR IGNORE INTO post_categories (post_id, category_id) VALUES (?, ?)'
);

const stmtById = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM posts WHERE id = ?');

const stmtFindByIdAggregated = db.prepare(`
  SELECT
    p.id, p.title, p.content, p.created_at, p.updated_at, p.user_id, p.status,
    u.username AS author,
    GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT c.name) AS categories,
    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN r.value = 1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) AS likes_count,
    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN r.value = -1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) AS dislikes_count,
    (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM comments cm WHERE cm.post_id = p.id AND cm.status = 'approved') AS comments_count
  FROM posts p
  JOIN users u ON u.id = p.user_id
  LEFT JOIN post_categories pc ON pc.post_id = p.id
  LEFT JOIN categories c ON c.id = pc.category_id
  LEFT JOIN reactions r ON r.target_type = 'post' AND r.target_id = p.id
  WHERE p.id = ?
  GROUP BY p.id
`);

const stmtUpdateStatus = db.prepare('UPDATE posts SET status = ? WHERE id = ?');

const stmtUpdateContent = db.prepare(`
  UPDATE posts 
  SET title = ?, content = ?, updated_at = strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%f', 'now')
  WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ? AND updated_at = ?
`);

const stmtDelete = db.prepare('DELETE FROM posts WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?');
const stmtDeleteCategories = db.prepare('DELETE FROM post_categories WHERE post_id = ?');

function buildListQuery({ category, filter, userId, includePending, limit, offset }) {
  const conditions = [];
  const params = [];

  if (!includePending) conditions.push("p.status = 'approved'");

  const base = `
    SELECT
      p.id, p.title, p.content, p.created_at, p.updated_at, p.user_id, p.status,
      u.username AS author,
      GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT c.name) AS categories,
      COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN r.value = 1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) AS likes_count,
      COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN r.value = -1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) AS dislikes_count,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM comments cm WHERE cm.post_id = p.id AND cm.status = 'approved') AS comments_count
    FROM posts p
    JOIN users u ON u.id = p.user_id
    LEFT JOIN post_categories pc ON pc.post_id = p.id
    LEFT JOIN categories c ON c.id = pc.category_id
    LEFT JOIN reactions r ON r.target_type = 'post' AND r.target_id = p.id
  `;

  if (category) {
    conditions.push(`p.id IN (SELECT pc2.post_id FROM post_categories pc2 JOIN categories c2 ON c2.id = pc2.category_id WHERE c2.name = ?)`);
    params.push(category);
  }
  if (filter === 'my_posts') {
    if (!userId) throw new Error('UNAUTHORIZED');
    conditions.push('p.user_id = ?');
    params.push(userId);
  } else if (filter === 'my_likes') {
    if (!userId) throw new Error('UNAUTHORIZED');
    conditions.push(`p.id IN (SELECT target_id FROM reactions WHERE target_type = 'post' AND value = 1 AND user_id = ?)`);
    params.push(userId);
  }

  const where = conditions.length ? `WHERE ${conditions.join(' AND ')}` : '';
  const query = `${base} ${where} GROUP BY p.id ORDER BY p.created_at DESC LIMIT ? OFFSET ?`;
  params.push(limit, offset);

  const countQuery = `SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT p.id) AS total FROM posts p ${where}`;
  const countParams = params.slice(0, params.length - 2);

  return { query, params, countQuery, countParams };
}

function list(opts) {
  const limit = Math.min(opts.limit || config.pagination.defaultSize, config.pagination.maxSize);
  const offset = opts.offset || 0;
  const { query, params, countQuery, countParams } = buildListQuery({
    category: opts.category, filter: opts.filter, userId: opts.userId,
    includePending: opts.includePending || false, limit, offset,
  });
  const rows = db.prepare(query).all(...params);
  const total = db.prepare(countQuery).get(...countParams).total;
  return { rows, total, limit, offset };
}

const createTx = db.transaction((userId, title, content, categoryIds, status) => {
  const info = stmtInsert.run(title, content, userId, status);
  const postId = info.lastInsertRowid;
  for (const cid of categoryIds) stmtAttachCategory.run(postId, cid);
  return postId;
});

const updateTx = db.transaction((postId, userId, title, content, categoryIds, expectedUpdatedAt) => {
  const info = stmtUpdateContent.run(title, content, postId, userId, expectedUpdatedAt);
  if (info.changes === 0) return false;

  stmtDeleteCategories.run(postId);
  for (const cid of categoryIds) stmtAttachCategory.run(postId, cid);
  return true;
});

const deleteTx = db.transaction((postId, userId) => {
  const info = stmtDelete.run(postId, userId);
  return info.changes > 0;
});

function listPending() {
  return db.prepare(`
    SELECT p.id, p.title, p.content, p.created_at, p.status, u.username AS author
    FROM posts p JOIN users u ON u.id = p.user_id
    WHERE p.status = 'pending' ORDER BY p.created_at ASC
  `).all();
}

module.exports = {
  findById: (id) => stmtById.get(id),
  findByIdAggregated: (id) => stmtFindByIdAggregated.get(id),
  list,
  create: (userId, title, content, categoryIds, status = 'approved') => createTx(userId, title, content, categoryIds, status),
  updateStatus: (id, status) => stmtUpdateStatus.run(status, id).changes > 0,
  update: (postId, userId, title, content, categoryIds, expectedUpdatedAt) => updateTx(postId, userId, title, content, categoryIds, expectedUpdatedAt),
  delete: (postId, userId) => deleteTx(postId, userId),
  listPending,
};


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\src\models\reaction.model.js ===
// ============================================================================
// src/models/reaction.model.js — Unified likes/dislikes
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// One row per (user, target_type, target_id).
// react() semantics:
//   - no existing row  → INSERT
//   - same value       → DELETE (toggle off)
//   - opposite value   → UPDATE (change like ↔ dislike)
// ============================================================================

const db = require('../database/db');

const stmtGet = db.prepare(`
  SELECT id, value FROM reactions
  WHERE user_id = ? AND target_type = ? AND target_id = ?
`);

const stmtInsert = db.prepare(`
  INSERT INTO reactions (user_id, target_type, target_id, value)
  VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
`);

const stmtUpdate = db.prepare('UPDATE reactions SET value = ? WHERE id = ?');
const stmtDelete = db.prepare('DELETE FROM reactions WHERE id = ?');

function react(userId, targetType, targetId, value) {
  if (value !== 1 && value !== -1) {
    throw new Error('value must be 1 or -1');
  }

  const existing = stmtGet.get(userId, targetType, targetId);

  if (!existing) {
    stmtInsert.run(userId, targetType, targetId, value);
    return { action: 'added', value };
  }

  if (existing.value === value) {
    stmtDelete.run(existing.id);
    return { action: 'removed', value };
  }

  stmtUpdate.run(value, existing.id);
  return { action: 'changed', value };
}

module.exports = { react };

=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\src\models\session.model.js ===
// ============================================================================
// src/models/session.model.js — F04 hardened + F05 roles JOIN
// ============================================================================

const db = require('../database/db');
const config = require('../config');
const { v4: uuidv4 } = require('uuid');

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Timestamp helpers
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// SQLite's CURRENT_TIMESTAMP returns UTC in 'YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS' format.
// JavaScript's Date parses that string as LOCAL time by default — which
// makes sessions on any machine with a non-UTC timezone expire hours early.
//
// parseUtc() normalizes both possible stored formats:
//   1. 'YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS'        (SQLite format, implicit UTC)
//   2. 'YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SS.sssZ'   (ISO 8601 with Z, explicit UTC)
// and returns a Date that always represents the correct UTC instant.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function toSqliteTimestamp(date) {
  return date.toISOString().replace('T', ' ').slice(0, 19);
}

function parseUtc(value) {
  if (!value) return null;
  const s = String(value);

  // ISO 8601 (contains 'T') — add Z if missing.
  if (s.includes('T')) {
    return new Date(s.endsWith('Z') ? s : s + 'Z');
  }

  // SQLite format — replace space with T and append Z to force UTC parsing.
  return new Date(s.replace(' ', 'T') + 'Z');
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Prepared statements
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const stmtInsert = db.prepare(`
  INSERT INTO sessions (id, user_id, expires_at, last_seen_at, user_agent_hash)
  VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
`);

// expires_at is stored in SQLite UTC format via toSqliteTimestamp(); comparing
// it against CURRENT_TIMESTAMP (also SQLite UTC) is a valid string compare
// and can still use idx_sessions_expires.
const stmtFind = db.prepare(`
  SELECT s.id, s.user_id, s.expires_at, s.created_at,
         s.last_seen_at, s.user_agent_hash, u.username, u.role
  FROM sessions s
  JOIN users u ON u.id = s.user_id
  WHERE s.id = ?
    AND s.expires_at > CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
`);

const stmtTouch = db.prepare(`
  UPDATE sessions
  SET last_seen_at = strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%fZ', 'now')
  WHERE id = ?
`);

const stmtDelete = db.prepare('DELETE FROM sessions WHERE id = ?');
const stmtDeleteExpired = db.prepare(
  'DELETE FROM sessions WHERE expires_at <= CURRENT_TIMESTAMP'
);
const stmtDeleteByUser = db.prepare('DELETE FROM sessions WHERE user_id = ?');

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Public API
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function create(userId, ttlMs, uaHash = null) {
  const id = uuidv4();
  const expiresAt = toSqliteTimestamp(new Date(Date.now() + ttlMs));
  const lastSeenAt = new Date().toISOString(); // ISO with Z (explicit UTC)
  stmtInsert.run(id, userId, expiresAt, lastSeenAt, uaHash);
  return { id, userId, expiresAt };
}

function findValid(id, uaHash = null) {
  const row = stmtFind.get(id);
  if (!row) return null;

  const now = Date.now();

  // 1. Absolute timeout — created_at is 'YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS' (UTC).
  const createdAt = parseUtc(row.created_at);
  if (createdAt && now - createdAt.getTime() > config.session.absoluteTimeoutMs) {
    stmtDelete.run(id);
    return null;
  }

  // 2. Idle timeout — last_seen_at is ISO 8601 with Z (UTC).
  const lastSeenAt = parseUtc(row.last_seen_at);
  if (lastSeenAt && now - lastSeenAt.getTime() > config.session.idleTimeoutMs) {
    stmtDelete.run(id);
    return null;
  }

  // 3. UA binding
  if (config.session.bindToUserAgent && row.user_agent_hash) {
    if (row.user_agent_hash !== uaHash) {
      stmtDelete.run(id);
      return null;
    }
  }

  // 4. Touch
  stmtTouch.run(id);
  return row;
}

function rotate(oldId, userId, ttlMs, uaHash = null) {
  if (oldId) stmtDelete.run(oldId);
  return create(userId, ttlMs, uaHash);
}

module.exports = {
  create,
  findValid,
  rotate,
  delete: (id) => stmtDelete.run(id),
  deleteExpired: () => stmtDeleteExpired.run(),
  deleteByUser: (userId) => stmtDeleteByUser.run(userId),
  deleteAllForUser: (userId) => stmtDeleteByUser.run(userId),
};

// ============================================================================
// src/routes/sso.routes.js — F02 SSO routes
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// GET /auth/providers              → public status (which providers enabled)
// GET /auth/:provider              → start flow (redirect to provider)
// GET /auth/:provider/callback     → handle callback
// ============================================================================

const express = require('express');
const router = express.Router();

const sso = require('../controllers/sso.controller');
const config = require('../config');
const { authLimiter } = require('../middleware/rateLimit');
const { HttpError } = require('../middleware/errorHandler');

// Reject disabled providers before hitting the controller.
function ensureEnabled(req, res, next) {
  const name = req.params.provider;
  const enabled =
    (name === 'google' && config.sso.google.enabled) ||
    (name === 'github' && config.sso.github.enabled);

  if (!enabled) {
    return next(new HttpError(404, 'Provider not enabled'));
  }
  next();
}

// Public status endpoint (frontend uses it to show/hide SSO buttons).
router.get('/auth/providers', sso.status);

// Start + callback. Rate-limited to prevent abuse.
router.get('/auth/:provider', ensureEnabled, authLimiter, sso.start);
router.get('/auth/:provider/callback', ensureEnabled, authLimiter, sso.callback);

module.exports = router;


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\src\utils\escapeHtml.js ===
// ============================================================================
// src/utils/escapeHtml.js — HTML escaping (defense against XSS)
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Use whenever user-controlled data is inserted into HTML.
// Frontend has its own copy in public/script.js.
// ============================================================================

function escapeHtml(value) {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return '';
  return String(value)
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
    .replace(/'/g, '&#39;')
    .replace(/\//g, '&#x2F;');
}

module.exports = { escapeHtml };


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\src\utils\hash.js ===
// ============================================================================
// src/utils/hash.js — SHA-256 helpers
// ============================================================================

const crypto = require('crypto');

function sha256Hex(buffer) {
  return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(buffer).digest('hex');
}

// Hash a string (e.g., user-agent) to a fixed-size fingerprint.
// 128 bits (32 hex chars) is more than enough for UA comparison.
function fingerprint(value) {
  if (!value) return null;
  return crypto
    .createHash('sha256')
    .update(String(value))
    .digest('hex')
    .slice(0, 32);
}

module.exports = { sha256Hex, fingerprint };


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\src\utils\logger.js ===
// ============================================================================
// src/utils/logger.js — Minimal structured logger
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Writes to stdout/stderr. No secrets, tokens, or request bodies logged.
// Container-friendly (Docker captures stdout/stderr).
// ============================================================================

const config = require('../config');

function ts() {
  return new Date().toISOString();
}

function log(level, msg, meta) {
  const line = `[${ts()}] [${level}] ${msg}`;
  const out = level === 'error' ? 'error' : 'log';

  if (meta !== undefined) {
    // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
    console[out](line, meta);
  } else {
    // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
    console[out](line);
  }
}

module.exports = {
  info: (msg, meta) => log('info', msg, meta),
  warn: (msg, meta) => log('warn', msg, meta),
  error: (msg, meta) => log('error', msg, meta),
};


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\src\utils\password.js ===
// ============================================================================
// src/utils/password.js — bcrypt hashing
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Cost factor 12 (~200ms per hash on modern hardware). This is the current
// recommendation from OWASP for password storage with bcrypt.
//
// SSO-only users have a password hash starting with '!' (unusable).
// verifyPassword() returns false for those, blocking password login.
// ============================================================================

const bcrypt = require('bcryptjs');

const ROUNDS = 12;

async function hashPassword(plain) {
  return bcrypt.hash(plain, ROUNDS);
}

async function verifyPassword(plain, hash) {
  if (!hash) return false;
  // SSO-only users have hashes starting with '!'; bcrypt would fail on them.
  if (hash.startsWith('!')) return false;
  return bcrypt.compare(plain, hash);
}

module.exports = { hashPassword, verifyPassword };


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\src\validators\auth.validator.js ===
// ============================================================================
// src/validators/auth.validator.js — Register / Login validation
// ============================================================================

const { HttpError } = require('../middleware/errorHandler');

const EMAIL_RE = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]{2,}$/;
const USERNAME_RE = /^[a-zA-Z0-9_]{3,30}$/;

function validateRegister(req) {
  const { username, email, password } = req.body || {};

  if (!username || !email || !password) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'All fields are required');
  }

  if (typeof username !== 'string' || !USERNAME_RE.test(username)) {
    throw new HttpError(
      400,
      'Username must be 3-30 chars: letters, digits, underscore'
    );
  }

  if (typeof email !== 'string' || !EMAIL_RE.test(email)) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid email format');
  }

  if (typeof password !== 'string' || password.length < 8 || password.length > 72) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Password must be 8-72 characters');
  }
}

function validateLogin(req) {
  const { username, password } = req.body || {};

  if (!username || !password) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Username and password required');
  }

  if (typeof username !== 'string' || typeof password !== 'string') {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid credentials payload');
  }

  if (username.length > 60 || password.length > 200) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid credentials payload');
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// validateChangePassword — POST /api/users/me/password
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function validateChangePassword(req) {
  const { currentPassword, newPassword } = req.body || {};

  if (!currentPassword || !newPassword) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Both currentPassword and newPassword are required');
  }

  if (typeof currentPassword !== 'string' || currentPassword.length === 0) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid current password');
  }

  if (typeof newPassword !== 'string' || newPassword.length < 8 || newPassword.length > 72) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'New password must be 8-72 characters');
  }

  if (currentPassword === newPassword) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'New password must differ from the current one');
  }
}

module.exports = { validateRegister, validateLogin, validateChangePassword };

const { HttpError } = require('../middleware/errorHandler');

function validateCreateComment(req) {
  const { content } = req.body || {};
  if (typeof content !== 'string' || content.trim().length < 1 || content.length > 2000) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Comment must be 1-2000 characters');
  }
}

function validateUpdateComment(req) {
  const { content, updatedAt } = req.body || {};
  if (typeof content !== 'string' || content.trim().length < 1 || content.length > 2000) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Comment must be 1-2000 characters');
  }
  if (!updatedAt || typeof updatedAt !== 'string') {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'updatedAt is required for edit (stale update protection)');
  }
}

module.exports = { validateCreateComment, validateUpdateComment };

// ============================================================================
// src/validators/moderation.validator.js — Moderation inputs
// ============================================================================

const { HttpError } = require('../middleware/errorHandler');

const VALID_ROLES = ['user', 'moderator', 'admin'];
const VALID_STATUSES = ['approved', 'pending', 'rejected'];

function validateRoleChange(req) {
  const { role } = req.body || {};
  if (!role || !VALID_ROLES.includes(role)) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Role must be one of: user, moderator, admin');
  }
}

function validateStatusChange(req) {
  const { status } = req.body || {};
  if (!status || !VALID_STATUSES.includes(status)) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Status must be one of: approved, pending, rejected');
  }
}

function validateReport(req) {
  const { targetType, targetId, reason } = req.body || {};
  if (!targetType || !['post', 'comment'].includes(targetType)) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'targetType must be post or comment');
  }
  if (!Number.isInteger(targetId) || targetId <= 0) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'targetId must be a positive integer');
  }
  if (typeof reason !== 'string' || reason.trim().length < 3 || reason.length > 500) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Reason must be 3-500 characters');
  }
}

function validateReportStatus(req) {
  const { status } = req.body || {};
  if (!status || !['resolved', 'dismissed'].includes(status)) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Status must be resolved or dismissed');
  }
}

function validateCreateCategory(req) {
  const { name } = req.body || {};
  if (typeof name !== 'string' || name.trim().length < 2 || name.length > 50) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Category name must be 2-50 characters');
  }
}

module.exports = {
  validateRoleChange,
  validateStatusChange,
  validateReport,
  validateReportStatus,
  validateCreateCategory,
};


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\src\validators\post.validator.js ===
// ============================================================================
// src/validators/post.validator.js — Create and Update post validation
// ============================================================================

const { HttpError } = require('../middleware/errorHandler');

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// validateCreatePost — POST /api/posts
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function validateCreatePost(req) {
  const { title, content, categoryIds } = req.body || {};

  if (typeof title !== 'string' || title.trim().length < 3 || title.length > 150) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Title must be 3-150 characters');
  }

  if (typeof content !== 'string' || content.trim().length < 1 || content.length > 5000) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Content must be 1-5000 characters');
  }

  if (!Array.isArray(categoryIds) || categoryIds.length === 0 || categoryIds.length > 10) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Select between 1 and 10 categories');
  }

  for (const id of categoryIds) {
    if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
      throw new HttpError(400, 'Invalid category id');
    }
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// validateUpdatePost — PUT /api/posts/:postId
// Includes updatedAt for optimistic locking (stale update protection).
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function validateUpdatePost(req) {
  const { title, content, categoryIds, updatedAt } = req.body || {};

  if (typeof title !== 'string' || title.trim().length < 3 || title.length > 150) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Title must be 3-150 characters');
  }
  
  if (typeof content !== 'string' || content.trim().length < 1 || content.length > 5000) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Content must be 1-5000 characters');
  }
  
  if (!Array.isArray(categoryIds) || categoryIds.length === 0 || categoryIds.length > 10) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'Select between 1 and 10 categories');
  }
  
  if (!updatedAt || typeof updatedAt !== 'string') {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'updatedAt is required for edit (stale update protection)');
  }
}

module.exports = { validateCreatePost, validateUpdatePost };

require('./setup');
const { resetDb, getCsrfFor, request } = require('./helpers');

let app;
let agent;
let csrf;

beforeAll(async () => {
  await resetDb(); // هذا يستدعي migrations و seed تلقائياً
  app = require('../src/app');
  agent = request.agent(app);
  const { token } = await getCsrfFor(agent);
  csrf = token;
});

describe('Auth', () => {
  test('register rejects missing fields', async () => {
    const res = await agent
      .post('/register')
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
      .send({});
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
  });

  test('register rejects invalid email', async () => {
    const res = await agent
      .post('/register')
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
      .send({ username: 'u1', email: 'not-an-email', password: 'longpassword' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
  });

  test('register rejects short password', async () => {
    const res = await agent
      .post('/register')
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
      .send({ username: 'u2', email: 'u2@example.com', password: 'short' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
  });

  test('register succeeds', async () => {
    const res = await agent
      .post('/register')
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
      .send({ username: 'alice', email: 'alice@example.com', password: 'longpassword' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(201);
    expect(res.body.username).toBe('alice');
  });

  test('register rejects duplicate email', async () => {
    const res = await agent
      .post('/register')
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
      .send({ username: 'alice2', email: 'alice@example.com', password: 'longpassword' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(409);
  });

  test('login fails with wrong password', async () => {
    const res = await agent
      .post('/login')
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
      .send({ username: 'alice', password: 'wrongpassword' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
  });

  test('login succeeds and sets session cookie', async () => {
    const res = await agent
      .post('/login')
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
      .send({ username: 'alice', password: 'longpassword' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    const cookies = res.headers['set-cookie'] || [];
    expect(cookies.some((c) => c.startsWith('forum_session='))).toBe(true);
  });

  test('GET /me unauthenticated', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/me');
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
    expect(res.body.loggedIn).toBe(false);
  });

  test('GET /me authenticated', async () => {
    const res = await agent.get('/me');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.loggedIn).toBe(true);
    expect(res.body.username).toBe('alice');
  });

  test('passwords are hashed in DB', async () => {
    const db = require('../src/database/db');
    const row = db.prepare('SELECT password FROM users WHERE username = ?').get('alice');
    expect(row.password).not.toBe('longpassword');
    expect(row.password.startsWith('$2')).toBe(true); // bcrypt prefix
  });

  test('logout clears session', async () => {
    const res = await agent
      .post('/logout')
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);

    const me = await agent.get('/me');
    expect(me.status).toBe(401);
  });
});

require('./setup');
const { resetDb, getCsrfFor, request } = require('./helpers');

let app, agent, csrf, postId;

beforeAll(async () => {
  await resetDb();
  app = require('../src/app');
  agent = request.agent(app);
  const { token } = await getCsrfFor(agent);
  csrf = token;

  await agent.post('/register').set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
    .send({ username: 'cuser', email: 'c@example.com', password: 'longpassword' });
  await agent.post('/login').set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
    .send({ username: 'cuser', password: 'longpassword' });

  const p = await agent.post('/api/posts').set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
    .send({ title: 'Post', content: 'Body', categoryIds: [1] });
  postId = p.body.id;
});

describe('Comments', () => {
  test('list comments is public', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get(`/api/posts/${postId}/comments`);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(Array.isArray(res.body)).toBe(true);
  });

  test('add comment requires CSRF (403)', async () => {
    const res = await request(app)
      .post(`/api/posts/${postId}/comments`)
      .send({ content: 'hi' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(403);
  });

  test('add comment requires auth after CSRF', async () => {
    const anonAgent = request.agent(app);
    const { token } = await getCsrfFor(anonAgent);
    const res = await anonAgent
      .post(`/api/posts/${postId}/comments`)
      .set('x-csrf-token', token)
      .send({ content: 'hi' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
  });

  test('add comment succeeds', async () => {
    const res = await agent
      .post(`/api/posts/${postId}/comments`)
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
      .send({ content: 'Nice post!' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(201);
    expect(res.body.id).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  test('comment appears in list', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get(`/api/posts/${postId}/comments`);
    expect(res.body.length).toBe(1);
    expect(res.body[0].content).toBe('Nice post!');
    expect(res.body[0].author).toBe('cuser');
  });

  test('rejects empty comment', async () => {
    const res = await agent
      .post(`/api/posts/${postId}/comments`)
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
      .send({ content: '   ' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
  });

  test('rejects comment on unknown post', async () => {
    const res = await agent
      .post('/api/posts/99999/comments')
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
      .send({ content: 'hi' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
  });
});


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\tests\f04.db-encryption.test.js ===
// ============================================================================
// tests/f04.db-encryption.test.js — P21: SQLCipher database encryption
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// These tests create a real encrypted DB file and verify:
//   1. Encryption works when a password is set
//   2. Wrong password is rejected
//   3. Raw file bytes do NOT contain plaintext
//   4. Unencrypted DB opens fine without a password
// ============================================================================

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

let Database;
let cipherAvailable = true;
try {
  // eslint-disable-next-line global-require
  Database = require('better-sqlite3-multiple-ciphers');
} catch (_) {
  cipherAvailable = false;
}

const describeIf = cipherAvailable ? describe : describe.skip;

const testDbPath = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'data', 'test-encrypted.db');
const plainDbPath = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'data', 'test-plain.db');
const PASSWORD = 'test-encryption-password-123';

function cleanup(p) {
  for (const suffix of ['', '-wal', '-shm']) {
    const f = p + suffix;
    if (fs.existsSync(f)) fs.unlinkSync(f);
  }
}

describeIf('F04/P21 — Database Encryption (SQLCipher)', () => {
  beforeAll(() => {
    const dataDir = path.dirname(testDbPath);
    if (!fs.existsSync(dataDir)) fs.mkdirSync(dataDir, { recursive: true });
    cleanup(testDbPath);
    cleanup(plainDbPath);
  });

  afterAll(() => {
    cleanup(testDbPath);
    cleanup(plainDbPath);
  });

  test('creates an encrypted DB and writes data', () => {
    const db = new Database(testDbPath);
    db.pragma(`key = '${PASSWORD}'`);
    db.exec('CREATE TABLE secret (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, data TEXT)');
    db.prepare('INSERT INTO secret (data) VALUES (?)').run('confidential');
    db.close();

    expect(fs.existsSync(testDbPath)).toBe(true);
    expect(fs.statSync(testDbPath).size).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  test('raw file does NOT contain plaintext', () => {
    const buffer = fs.readFileSync(testDbPath);
    const text = buffer.toString('utf8');
    expect(text).not.toContain('confidential');
    expect(text).not.toContain('CREATE TABLE secret');
  });

  test('opens with correct password', () => {
    const db = new Database(testDbPath);
    db.pragma(`key = '${PASSWORD}'`);
    const row = db.prepare('SELECT data FROM secret').get();
    expect(row.data).toBe('confidential');
    db.close();
  });

  test('rejects wrong password', () => {
    const db = new Database(testDbPath);
    db.pragma(`key = 'wrong-password-xyz'`);
    expect(() => {
      db.prepare('SELECT data FROM secret').get();
    }).toThrow();
    db.close();
  });

  test('rejects no password', () => {
    const db = new Database(testDbPath);
    expect(() => {
      db.prepare('SELECT data FROM secret').get();
    }).toThrow();
    db.close();
  });

  test('plaintext DB opens fine without a password', () => {
    const db = new Database(plainDbPath);
    db.exec('CREATE TABLE plain (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, v TEXT)');
    db.prepare('INSERT INTO plain (v) VALUES (?)').run('hello');
    db.close();

    const db2 = new Database(plainDbPath);
    const row = db2.prepare('SELECT v FROM plain').get();
    expect(row.v).toBe('hello');
    db2.close();
  });
});

require('./setup');
const { resetDb, getCsrfFor, request } = require('./helpers');

let app;

beforeAll(async () => {
  await resetDb();
  app = require('../src/app');
});

describe('F04 — Password Change', () => {
  async function setupUser(username) {
    const agent = request.agent(app);
    const { token } = await getCsrfFor(agent);
    await agent.post('/register')
      .set('x-csrf-token', token)
      .send({ username, email: `${username}@e.com`, password: 'oldpassword123' });
    await agent.post('/login')
      .set('x-csrf-token', token)
      .send({ username, password: 'oldpassword123' });
    return { agent, token };
  }

  test('requires authentication', async () => {
    const anon = request.agent(app);
    const { token } = await getCsrfFor(anon);
    const res = await anon.post('/api/users/me/password')
      .set('x-csrf-token', token)
      .send({ currentPassword: 'oldpassword123', newPassword: 'newpassword456' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
  });

  test('requires correct current password', async () => {
    const { agent, token } = await setupUser('pwtest1');
    const res = await agent.post('/api/users/me/password')
      .set('x-csrf-token', token)
      .send({ currentPassword: 'wrongpassword', newPassword: 'newpassword456' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
    expect(res.body.error).toMatch(/current password/i);
  });

  test('rejects new password shorter than 8 chars', async () => {
    const { agent, token } = await setupUser('pwtest2');
    const res = await agent.post('/api/users/me/password')
      .set('x-csrf-token', token)
      .send({ currentPassword: 'oldpassword123', newPassword: 'short' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
  });

  test('rejects new password equal to current', async () => {
    const { agent, token } = await setupUser('pwtest3');
    const res = await agent.post('/api/users/me/password')
      .set('x-csrf-token', token)
      .send({ currentPassword: 'oldpassword123', newPassword: 'oldpassword123' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
    expect(res.body.error).toMatch(/differ/i);
  });

  test('successful change rotates session and keeps user logged in', async () => {
    const { agent, token } = await setupUser('pwtest4');

    const db = require('../src/database/db');
    const user = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = ?').get('pwtest4');
    const oldSession = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM sessions WHERE user_id = ?').get(user.id);
    expect(oldSession).toBeTruthy();

    const res = await agent.post('/api/users/me/password')
      .set('x-csrf-token', token)
      .send({ currentPassword: 'oldpassword123', newPassword: 'newpassword789' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.ok).toBe(true);

    const oldStill = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM sessions WHERE id = ?').get(oldSession.id);
    expect(oldStill).toBeUndefined();

    const newSession = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM sessions WHERE user_id = ?').get(user.id);
    expect(newSession).toBeTruthy();
    expect(newSession.id).not.toBe(oldSession.id);

    const me = await agent.get('/me');
    expect(me.status).toBe(200);
  });

  test('old password no longer works after change', async () => {
    const { agent, token } = await setupUser('pwtest5');

    await agent.post('/api/users/me/password')
      .set('x-csrf-token', token)
      .send({ currentPassword: 'oldpassword123', newPassword: 'newpassword789' });
    await agent.post('/logout').set('x-csrf-token', token);

    const freshAgent = request.agent(app);
    const { token: t2 } = await getCsrfFor(freshAgent);
    const oldLogin = await freshAgent.post('/login')
      .set('x-csrf-token', t2)
      .send({ username: 'pwtest5', password: 'oldpassword123' });
    expect(oldLogin.status).toBe(401);

    const newLogin = await freshAgent.post('/login')
      .set('x-csrf-token', t2)
      .send({ username: 'pwtest5', password: 'newpassword789' });
    expect(newLogin.status).toBe(200);
  });
});

require('./setup');
const { resetDb, getCsrfFor, request } = require('./helpers');

let app;

beforeAll(async () => {
  await resetDb();
  app = require('../src/app');
});

describe('F04 — Rate Limiting', () => {
  test('login blocked after 10 attempts for same username', async () => {
    const regAgent = request.agent(app);
    const { token: regToken } = await getCsrfFor(regAgent);
    await regAgent.post('/register')
      .set('x-csrf-token', regToken)
      .send({ username: 'limituser', email: 'limit@e.com', password: 'longpassword' });

    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
      const agent = request.agent(app);
      const { token } = await getCsrfFor(agent);
      const res = await agent.post('/login')
        .set('x-csrf-token', token)
        .send({ username: 'limituser', password: 'wrongpassword' });
      expect(res.status).toBe(401);
    }

    const agent = request.agent(app);
    const { token } = await getCsrfFor(agent);
    const blocked = await agent.post('/login')
      .set('x-csrf-token', token)
      .send({ username: 'limituser', password: 'longpassword' });
    expect(blocked.status).toBe(429);
  });

  test('different usernames have independent login limits', async () => {
    const regAgent = request.agent(app);
    const { token: regToken } = await getCsrfFor(regAgent);
    await regAgent.post('/register')
      .set('x-csrf-token', regToken)
      .send({ username: 'otheruser', email: 'other@e.com', password: 'longpassword' });

    const agent = request.agent(app);
    const { token } = await getCsrfFor(agent);
    const res = await agent.post('/login')
      .set('x-csrf-token', token)
      .send({ username: 'otheruser', password: 'longpassword' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
  });

  test('post creation blocked after 10 posts within window', async () => {
    const agent = request.agent(app);
    const { token } = await getCsrfFor(agent);

    await agent.post('/register')
      .set('x-csrf-token', token)
      .send({ username: 'spammer', email: 'spam@e.com', password: 'longpassword' });
    await agent.post('/login')
      .set('x-csrf-token', token)
      .send({ username: 'spammer', password: 'longpassword' });

    for (let i = 1; i <= 10; i++) {
      const res = await agent.post('/api/posts')
        .set('x-csrf-token', token)
        .send({ title: `Post ${i}`, content: `Content ${i}`, categoryIds: [1] });
      expect(res.status).toBe(201);
    }

    const blocked = await agent.post('/api/posts')
      .set('x-csrf-token', token)
      .send({ title: 'Post 11', content: 'Blocked', categoryIds: [1] });
    expect(blocked.status).toBe(429);
  });

  test('comment creation blocked after 20 comments', async () => {
    const agent = request.agent(app);
    const { token } = await getCsrfFor(agent);

    await agent.post('/register')
      .set('x-csrf-token', token)
      .send({ username: 'chatty', email: 'chatty@e.com', password: 'longpassword' });
    await agent.post('/login')
      .set('x-csrf-token', token)
      .send({ username: 'chatty', password: 'longpassword' });

    const post = await agent.post('/api/posts')
      .set('x-csrf-token', token)
      .send({ title: 'Target post', content: 'Body', categoryIds: [1] });
    const postId = post.body.id;

    for (let i = 1; i <= 20; i++) {
      const res = await agent.post(`/api/posts/${postId}/comments`)
        .set('x-csrf-token', token)
        .send({ content: `Comment ${i}` });
      expect(res.status).toBe(201);
    }

    const blocked = await agent.post(`/api/posts/${postId}/comments`)
      .set('x-csrf-token', token)
      .send({ content: 'Blocked' });
    expect(blocked.status).toBe(429);
  });
});


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\tests\f04.session.test.js ===
require('./setup');
const { resetDb, getCsrfFor, request } = require('./helpers');

let app;

beforeAll(async () => {
  await resetDb();
  app = require('../src/app');
});

describe('F04 — Session Hardening', () => {
  const UA = 'Mozilla/5.0 TestBrowser';

  async function registerAndLogin(agent, username, ua = UA) {
    const { token } = await getCsrfFor(agent);
    await agent.post('/register')
      .set('x-csrf-token', token)
      .set('User-Agent', ua)
      .send({ username, email: `${username}@e.com`, password: 'longpassword' });
    await agent.post('/login')
      .set('x-csrf-token', token)
      .set('User-Agent', ua)
      .send({ username, password: 'longpassword' });
    return token;
  }

  test('login creates a session with user_agent_hash', async () => {
    const agent = request.agent(app);
    await registerAndLogin(agent, 'alice');

    const db = require('../src/database/db');
    const s = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM sessions ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1').get();
    expect(s).toBeTruthy();
    expect(s.user_agent_hash).toBeTruthy();
    expect(s.user_agent_hash).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{32}$/);
    expect(s.last_seen_at).toBeTruthy();
  });

  test('login from a second device kills the first session', async () => {
    const db = require('../src/database/db');
    
    // Clear sessions to isolate this test
    db.prepare('DELETE FROM sessions').run();

    const deviceA = request.agent(app);
    await registerAndLogin(deviceA, 'bob', 'DeviceA/1.0');

    const countAfterA = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM sessions').get().c;
    expect(countAfterA).toBe(1);

    const deviceB = request.agent(app);
    const { token } = await getCsrfFor(deviceB);
    await deviceB.post('/login')
      .set('x-csrf-token', token)
      .set('User-Agent', 'DeviceB/1.0')
      .send({ username: 'bob', password: 'longpassword' });

    const countAfterB = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM sessions').get().c;
    expect(countAfterB).toBe(1); // old session deleted

    // Device A should now be logged out (401) - passing correct UA
    const meA = await deviceA.get('/me').set('User-Agent', 'DeviceA/1.0');
    expect(meA.status).toBe(401);

    // Device B should be logged in (200) - passing correct UA
    const meB = await deviceB.get('/me').set('User-Agent', 'DeviceB/1.0');
    expect(meB.status).toBe(200);
  });

  test('session rejected when UA changes (hijacking defense)', async () => {
    const agent = request.agent(app);
    await registerAndLogin(agent, 'carol', 'OriginalBrowser/1.0');

    // Normal request with same UA → 200
    const ok = await agent.get('/me').set('User-Agent', 'OriginalBrowser/1.0');
    expect(ok.status).toBe(200);

    // Same session, different UA → 401
    const hijacked = await agent.get('/me').set('User-Agent', 'DifferentBrowser/2.0');
    expect(hijacked.status).toBe(401);

    // Session was deleted from DB
    const db = require('../src/database/db');
    const u = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = ?').get('carol');
    const s = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM sessions WHERE user_id = ?').get(u.id);
    expect(s).toBeUndefined();
  });

  test('session revoked after idle timeout', async () => {
    const config = require('../src/config');
    const originalIdle = config.session.idleTimeoutMs;

    try {
      // 2000ms > bcrypt time (~200ms) to prevent premature expiry
      config.session.idleTimeoutMs = 2000;

      const agent = request.agent(app);
      await registerAndLogin(agent, 'dave');

      const now = await agent.get('/me').set('User-Agent', UA);
      expect(now.status).toBe(200);

      // Wait > idle timeout
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 2100));

      const afterIdle = await agent.get('/me').set('User-Agent', UA);
      expect(afterIdle.status).toBe(401);
    } finally {
      config.session.idleTimeoutMs = originalIdle;
    }
  });

  test('session revoked after absolute timeout', async () => {
    const config = require('../src/config');
    const originalAbs = config.session.absoluteTimeoutMs;

    try {
      config.session.absoluteTimeoutMs = 2000;
      const agent = request.agent(app);
      await registerAndLogin(agent, 'eve');

      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 2100));

      const afterAbs = await agent.get('/me').set('User-Agent', UA);
      expect(afterAbs.status).toBe(401);
    } finally {
      config.session.absoluteTimeoutMs = originalAbs;
    }
  });

  test('logout deletes the session from DB', async () => {
    const agent = request.agent(app);
    const token = await registerAndLogin(agent, 'frank');

    const before = await agent.get('/me').set('User-Agent', UA);
    expect(before.status).toBe(200);

    await agent.post('/logout')
      .set('x-csrf-token', token)
      .set('User-Agent', UA);
      
    const after = await agent.get('/me').set('User-Agent', UA);
    expect(after.status).toBe(401);
  });
});
