require('./setup');
const { resetDb, getCsrfFor, request } = require('./helpers');

let app;

beforeAll(async () => {
  await resetDb();
  app = require('../src/app');
});

describe('F05 — Moderation', () => {
  let adminAgent, adminToken;
  let userAgent, userToken;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    // 1. First user (Admin)
    adminAgent = request.agent(app);
    const { token: aT } = await getCsrfFor(adminAgent);
    adminToken = aT;
    await adminAgent.post('/register')
      .set('x-csrf-token', adminToken)
      .send({ username: 'admin', email: 'admin@e.com', password: 'longpassword' });
    await adminAgent.post('/login')
      .set('x-csrf-token', adminToken)
      .send({ username: 'admin', password: 'longpassword' });

    // 2. Second user (Regular)
    userAgent = request.agent(app);
    const { token: uT } = await getCsrfFor(userAgent);
    userToken = uT;
    await userAgent.post('/register')
      .set('x-csrf-token', userToken)
      .send({ username: 'user1', email: 'user1@e.com', password: 'longpassword' });
    await userAgent.post('/login')
      .set('x-csrf-token', userToken)
      .send({ username: 'user1', password: 'longpassword' });
  });

  test('first user is admin, second is user', async () => {
    const db = require('../src/database/db');
    const a = db.prepare('SELECT role FROM users WHERE username = ?').get('admin');
    const u = db.prepare('SELECT role FROM users WHERE username = ?').get('user1');
    expect(a.role).toBe('admin');
    expect(u.role).toBe('user');
  });

  test('user cannot access moderation queue', async () => {
    const res = await userAgent.get('/api/moderation/queue');
    expect(res.status).toBe(403);
  });

  test('admin promotes user1 to moderator', async () => {
    const db = require('../src/database/db');
    const u = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = ?').get('user1');

    const res = await adminAgent.patch(`/api/users/${u.id}/role`)
      .set('x-csrf-token', adminToken)
      .send({ role: 'moderator' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);

    const updated = db.prepare('SELECT role FROM users WHERE id = ?').get(u.id);
    expect(updated.role).toBe('moderator');
  });

  test('moderator can access queue', async () => {
    const res = await userAgent.get('/api/moderation/queue');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body).toHaveProperty('posts');
    expect(res.body).toHaveProperty('comments');
  });

  test('moderator approves a pending post', async () => {
    const db = require('../src/database/db');
    const postModel = require('../src/models/post.model');
    const u = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = ?').get('user1');
    
    // Create a post with 'pending' status
    const postId = postModel.create(u.id, 'Pending title', 'Pending content', [1], 'pending');

    // Queue contains it
    const q1 = await userAgent.get('/api/moderation/queue');
    expect(q1.body.posts.some(p => p.id === postId)).toBe(true);

    // Approve it
    const res = await userAgent.patch(`/api/moderation/posts/${postId}/status`)
      .set('x-csrf-token', userToken)
      .send({ status: 'approved' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);

    const p = db.prepare('SELECT status FROM posts WHERE id = ?').get(postId);
    expect(p.status).toBe('approved');
  });

  test('user creates report, moderator resolves it', async () => {
    const db = require('../src/database/db');
    const post = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM posts LIMIT 1').get();
    
    // Create
    const createRes = await userAgent.post('/api/reports')
      .set('x-csrf-token', userToken)
      .send({ targetType: 'post', targetId: post.id, reason: 'Spam' });
    expect(createRes.status).toBe(201);
    const reportId = createRes.body.id;

    // List
    const listRes = await userAgent.get('/api/moderation/reports');
    expect(listRes.status).toBe(200);
    expect(listRes.body.some(r => r.id === reportId)).toBe(true);

    // Resolve
    const resolveRes = await userAgent.patch(`/api/moderation/reports/${reportId}`)
      .set('x-csrf-token', userToken)
      .send({ status: 'resolved' });
    expect(resolveRes.status).toBe(200);
    
    const r = db.prepare('SELECT status FROM reports WHERE id = ?').get(reportId);
    expect(r.status).toBe('resolved');
  });
});

require('./setup');
const { resetDb, getCsrfFor, request } = require('./helpers');

let app;

beforeAll(async () => {
  await resetDb();
  app = require('../src/app');
});

describe('F06 — Advanced Features', () => {
  let userA, tokenA, userB, tokenB, postId;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    // User A
    userA = request.agent(app);
    const { token: ta } = await getCsrfFor(userA);
    tokenA = ta;
    await userA.post('/register').set('x-csrf-token', tokenA)
      .send({ username: 'usera', email: 'a@e.com', password: 'longpassword' });
    await userA.post('/login').set('x-csrf-token', tokenA)
      .send({ username: 'usera', password: 'longpassword' });

    // User B
    userB = request.agent(app);
    const { token: tb } = await getCsrfFor(userB);
    tokenB = tb;
    await userB.post('/register').set('x-csrf-token', tokenB)
      .send({ username: 'userb', email: 'b@e.com', password: 'longpassword' });
    await userB.post('/login').set('x-csrf-token', tokenB)
      .send({ username: 'userb', password: 'longpassword' });
  });

  test('create post logs activity', async () => {
    const res = await userA.post('/api/posts').set('x-csrf-token', tokenA)
      .send({ title: 'First Post', content: 'Hello', categoryIds: [1] });
    expect(res.status).toBe(201);
    postId = res.body.id;

    const act = await userA.get('/api/activity/me');
    expect(act.status).toBe(200);
    expect(act.body.some(a => a.action === 'post_create' && a.target_id === postId)).toBe(true);
  });

  test('edit post updates content and logs activity', async () => {
    const post = await userA.get('/api/posts?page=1&pageSize=100');
    const p = post.body.data.find(x => x.id === postId);

    const res = await userA.put(`/api/posts/${postId}`).set('x-csrf-token', tokenA)
      .send({
        title: 'First Post (edited)',
        content: 'Updated',
        categoryIds: [1],
        updatedAt: p.updated_at,
      });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);

    const act = await userA.get('/api/activity/me');
    expect(act.body.some(a => a.action === 'post_edit' && a.target_id === postId)).toBe(true);
  });

  test('stale update rejected (409)', async () => {
    const post = await userA.get('/api/posts?page=1&pageSize=100');
    const p = post.body.data.find(x => x.id === postId);

    // First edit succeeds
    await userA.put(`/api/posts/${postId}`).set('x-csrf-token', tokenA)
      .send({
        title: 'Edit 1',
        content: 'V1',
        categoryIds: [1],
        updatedAt: p.updated_at,
      });

    // Second edit with OLD updatedAt → 409
    const res = await userA.put(`/api/posts/${postId}`).set('x-csrf-token', tokenA)
      .send({
        title: 'Edit 2',
        content: 'V2',
        categoryIds: [1],
        updatedAt: p.updated_at, // stale
      });
    expect(res.status).toBe(409);
  });

  test('other user cannot edit someone else\'s post', async () => {
    const post = await userB.get('/api/posts?page=1&pageSize=100');
    const p = post.body.data.find(x => x.id === postId);

    const res = await userB.put(`/api/posts/${postId}`).set('x-csrf-token', tokenB)
      .send({
        title: 'Hijacked',
        content: 'Nope',
        categoryIds: [1],
        updatedAt: p.updated_at,
      });
    expect(res.status).toBe(403);
  });

  test('comment triggers notification to post owner', async () => {
    // User B comments on User A's post
    await userB.post(`/api/posts/${postId}/comments`).set('x-csrf-token', tokenB)
      .send({ content: 'Nice post!' });

    // User A should have 1 unread notification
    const notif = await userA.get('/api/notifications');
    expect(notif.status).toBe(200);
    expect(notif.body.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(notif.body[0].type).toBe('comment');
    expect(notif.body[0].is_read).toBe(0);

    // Mark as read
    const mark = await userA.patch(`/api/notifications/${notif.body[0].id}/read`)
      .set('x-csrf-token', tokenA);
    expect(mark.status).toBe(200);
  });

  test('like triggers notification but self-like does not', async () => {
    // User B likes A's post
    await userB.post(`/api/posts/${postId}/like`).set('x-csrf-token', tokenB);
    const notif = await userA.get('/api/notifications');
    expect(notif.body.some(n => n.type === 'like')).toBe(true);

    // User A likes own post → no notification
    await userA.post(`/api/posts/${postId}/like`).set('x-csrf-token', tokenA);
    const notif2 = await userA.get('/api/notifications');
    const selfLikes = notif2.body.filter(n => n.type === 'like' && n.actor_name === 'usera');
    expect(selfLikes.length).toBe(0);
  });

  test('only owner can delete post', async () => {
    const res = await userB.delete(`/api/posts/${postId}`).set('x-csrf-token', tokenB);
    expect(res.status).toBe(403);
  });

  test('owner can delete own post', async () => {
    const res = await userA.delete(`/api/posts/${postId}`).set('x-csrf-token', tokenA);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);

    const get = await request(app).get(`/api/posts/${postId}`);
    // 404 because it's deleted (postModel.findById returns undefined, list won't include it)
    const list = await request(app).get('/api/posts');
    expect(list.body.data.some(p => p.id === postId)).toBe(false);
  });
});


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\tests\helpers.js ===
// ============================================================================
// tests/helpers.js — Shared test utilities
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
//   resetDb()      — fresh in-memory DB for this test file
//   getCsrfFor()   — fetch a CSRF token + cookies via a safe GET
//   request        — re-export supertest
// ============================================================================

const request = require('supertest');

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// resetDb — fresh in-memory DB for this test file
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// jest.resetModules() clears Jest's module registry (Node's require.cache
// is NOT enough). The next require() re-executes every module, so db.js
// opens a brand-new :memory: database. Migrations and seed run immediately
// after, so the test file starts with a clean, seeded schema.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async function resetDb() {
  // Ensure memory mode + isolated media root.
  process.env.DB_PATH = ':memory:';
  process.env.MEDIA_ROOT = './media/test';

  // Clear Jest's module registry so the next require() starts fresh.
  jest.resetModules();

  // Re-require the freshly reset schema and seed modules.
  const { runMigrations } = require('../src/database/schema');
  runMigrations();

  const { seed } = require('../src/database/seed');
  seed();
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// getCsrfFor — obtain a CSRF token + cookie string from a safe GET
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async function getCsrfFor(agentOrApp) {
  const res = await agentOrApp.get('/auth/providers');

  const setCookie = res.headers['set-cookie'] || [];
  const csrfCookie = setCookie.find((c) => c.startsWith('csrf_token='));
  const sessionCookie = setCookie.find((c) => c.startsWith('forum_session='));

  const cookies = [csrfCookie, sessionCookie]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .map((c) => c.split(';')[0])
    .join('; ');

  const token = csrfCookie ? csrfCookie.split(';')[0].split('=')[1] : null;

  return { token, cookies };
}

module.exports = { resetDb, getCsrfFor, request };

require('./setup');
const sharp = require('sharp');
const { resetDb, getCsrfFor, request } = require('./helpers');

let app, agent, csrf, postId, mediaId;

async function makePng() {
  return sharp({
    create: { width: 50, height: 50, channels: 3, background: { r: 0, g: 0, b: 255 } },
  }).png().toBuffer();
}

beforeAll(async () => {
  await resetDb();
  app = require('../src/app');
  agent = request.agent(app);
  const { token } = await getCsrfFor(agent);
  csrf = token;

  await agent.post('/register').set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
    .send({ username: 'lcuser', email: 'lc@e.com', password: 'longpassword' });
  await agent.post('/login').set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
    .send({ username: 'lcuser', password: 'longpassword' });

  const p = await agent.post('/api/posts').set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
    .send({ title: 'Lifecycle test post', content: 'Body content', categoryIds: [1] });
  postId = p.body.id;

  const up = await agent.post(`/api/posts/${postId}/media`)
    .set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
    .attach('file', await makePng(), 'x.png');
  mediaId = up.body.id;
});

describe('Media lifecycle', () => {
  test('owner can delete own media', async () => {
    const res = await agent.delete(`/api/media/${mediaId}`).set('x-csrf-token', csrf);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    const after = await request(app).get(`/api/media/${mediaId}`);
    expect(after.status).toBe(404);
  });

  test('non-owner cannot delete', async () => {
    const up = await agent.post(`/api/posts/${postId}/media`)
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
      .attach('file', await makePng(), 'y.png');
    const id = up.body.id;

    const other = request.agent(app);
    const { token: t2 } = await getCsrfFor(other);
    await other.post('/register').set('x-csrf-token', t2)
      .send({ username: 'other', email: 'o@e.com', password: 'longpassword' });
    await other.post('/login').set('x-csrf-token', t2)
      .send({ username: 'other', password: 'longpassword' });

    const res = await other.delete(`/api/media/${id}`).set('x-csrf-token', t2);
    expect(res.status).toBe(403);
  });

  test('list media for post returns uploaded items', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get(`/api/posts/${postId}/media`);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(Array.isArray(res.body)).toBe(true);
    expect(res.body.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
});

require('./setup');
const { resetDb, getCsrfFor, request } = require('./helpers');

let app, agent, csrf, postId;

beforeAll(async () => {
  await resetDb();
  app = require('../src/app');
  agent = request.agent(app);
  const { token } = await getCsrfFor(agent);
  csrf = token;

  await agent.post('/register').set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
    .send({ username: 'secuser', email: 'sec@e.com', password: 'longpassword' });
  await agent.post('/login').set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
    .send({ username: 'secuser', password: 'longpassword' });

  const p = await agent.post('/api/posts').set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
    .send({ title: 'Security test post', content: 'Body content', categoryIds: [1] });
  postId = p.body.id;
});

describe('Media security', () => {
  test('rejects non-image file (exe renamed)', async () => {
    const buf = Buffer.from('MZ\x90\x00\x03\x00\x00\x00', 'binary');
    const res = await agent.post(`/api/posts/${postId}/media`)
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
      .attach('file', buf, 'not_really.jpg');
    expect(res.status).toBe(415);
  });

  test('rejects SVG (XSS vector)', async () => {
    const svg = Buffer.from(
      '<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" onload="alert(1)"></svg>'
    );
    const res = await agent.post(`/api/posts/${postId}/media`)
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
      .attach('file', svg, 'x.svg');
    expect(res.status).toBe(415);
  });

  test('rejects text file with image extension', async () => {
    const buf = Buffer.from('hello world', 'utf8');
    const res = await agent.post(`/api/posts/${postId}/media`)
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
      .attach('file', buf, 'fake.png');
    expect([400, 415]).toContain(res.status);
  });

  test('rejects empty file', async () => {
    const res = await agent.post(`/api/posts/${postId}/media`)
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
      .attach('file', Buffer.alloc(0), 'empty.png');
    expect([400, 415]).toContain(res.status);
  });
});

require('./setup');
const path = require('path');
const fs = require('fs');
const sharp = require('sharp');
const { resetDb, getCsrfFor, request } = require('./helpers');

let app, agent, csrf, postId;

async function makePng() {
  return sharp({
    create: { width: 200, height: 200, channels: 3, background: { r: 255, g: 0, b: 0 } },
  }).png().toBuffer();
}

beforeAll(async () => {
  await resetDb();
  app = require('../src/app');
  agent = request.agent(app);
  const { token } = await getCsrfFor(agent);
  csrf = token;

  await agent.post('/register').set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
    .send({ username: 'muser', email: 'm@e.com', password: 'longpassword' });
  await agent.post('/login').set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
    .send({ username: 'muser', password: 'longpassword' });

  const p = await agent.post('/api/posts').set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
    .send({ title: 'Post with media', content: 'Body content', categoryIds: [1] });
  postId = p.body.id;

  const root = path.resolve('./media/test');
  if (fs.existsSync(root)) fs.rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

describe('Media upload', () => {
  test('rejects upload without auth (401 after CSRF)', async () => {
    const anon = request.agent(app);
    const { token } = await getCsrfFor(anon);
    const buf = await makePng();
    const res = await anon
      .post(`/api/posts/${postId}/media`)
      .set('x-csrf-token', token)
      .attach('file', buf, 'a.png');
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
  });

  test('rejects upload without CSRF (403)', async () => {
    const buf = await makePng();
    const res = await agent.post(`/api/posts/${postId}/media`)
      .attach('file', buf, 'a.png');
    expect(res.status).toBe(403);
  });

  test('accepts valid PNG and returns URL', async () => {
    const buf = await makePng();
    const res = await agent.post(`/api/posts/${postId}/media`)
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
      .attach('file', buf, 'a.png');
    expect(res.status).toBe(201);
    expect(res.body.url).toMatch(/^\/api\/media\/\d+$/);
    expect(res.body.width).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(res.body.height).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  test('rejects file over size limit (413)', async () => {
    const big = Buffer.alloc(6 * 1024 * 1024, 0xff);
    const res = await agent.post(`/api/posts/${postId}/media`)
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
      .attach('file', big, 'huge.png');
    expect([400, 413]).toContain(res.status);
  });

  test('serves uploaded media with correct headers', async () => {
    const buf = await makePng();
    const up = await agent.post(`/api/posts/${postId}/media`)
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
      .attach('file', buf, 'serve.png');
    const res = await request(app).get(up.body.url);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.headers['x-content-type-options']).toBe('nosniff');
    expect(res.headers['content-type']).toMatch(/^image\//);
  });
});

require('./setup');
const { resetDb, getCsrfFor, request } = require('./helpers');

let app, agent, csrf, postId;

beforeAll(async () => {
  await resetDb();
  app = require('../src/app');
  agent = request.agent(app);
  const { token } = await getCsrfFor(agent);
  csrf = token;

  await agent.post('/register').set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
    .send({ username: 'spostuser', email: 'spost@e.com', password: 'longpassword' });
  await agent.post('/login').set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
    .send({ username: 'spostuser', password: 'longpassword' });

  const p = await agent.post('/api/posts').set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
    .send({ title: 'Single post test', content: 'Body', categoryIds: [1] });
  postId = p.body.id;
});

describe('GET /api/posts/:postId', () => {
  test('returns post with categories array', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get(`/api/posts/${postId}`);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.id).toBe(postId);
    expect(Array.isArray(res.body.categories)).toBe(true);
    expect(res.body.categories.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(res.body.updated_at).toBeTruthy();
    expect(res.body.author).toBe('spostuser');
  });

  test('returns 404 for unknown post', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/api/posts/99999');
    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
  });

  test('returns 400 for invalid id', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/api/posts/abc');
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
  });
});


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\tests\posts.test.js ===
require('./setup');
const { resetDb, getCsrfFor, request } = require('./helpers');

let app, agent, csrf;

beforeAll(async () => {
  await resetDb();
  app = require('../src/app');
  agent = request.agent(app);
  const { token } = await getCsrfFor(agent);
  csrf = token;

  await agent.post('/register').set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
    .send({ username: 'puser', email: 'p@example.com', password: 'longpassword' });
  await agent.post('/login').set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
    .send({ username: 'puser', password: 'longpassword' });
});

describe('Posts', () => {
  test('list posts is public', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/api/posts');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(Array.isArray(res.body.data)).toBe(true);
    expect(res.body.pagination).toBeDefined();
  });

  test('create post requires CSRF (403)', async () => {
    const res = await request(app)
      .post('/api/posts')
      .send({ title: 'Title', content: 'Body', categoryIds: [1] });
    // CSRF middleware runs before requireAuth → 403
    expect(res.status).toBe(403);
  });

  test('create post requires auth after CSRF', async () => {
    const anonAgent = request.agent(app);
    const { token } = await getCsrfFor(anonAgent);
    const res = await anonAgent
      .post('/api/posts')
      .set('x-csrf-token', token)
      .send({ title: 'Title', content: 'Body', categoryIds: [1] });
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
  });

  test('create post rejects missing categories', async () => {
    const res = await agent
      .post('/api/posts')
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
      .send({ title: 'Hello', content: 'World', categoryIds: [] });
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
  });

  test('create post with valid data succeeds', async () => {
    const res = await agent
      .post('/api/posts')
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
      .send({ title: 'Hello', content: 'World', categoryIds: [1] });
    expect(res.status).toBe(201);
    expect(res.body.id).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  test('rejects unknown category id', async () => {
    const res = await agent
      .post('/api/posts')
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
      .send({ title: 'X', content: 'Y', categoryIds: [99999] });
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
  });

  test('rejects too-long content', async () => {
    const res = await agent
      .post('/api/posts')
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
      .send({ title: 'A', content: 'x'.repeat(6000), categoryIds: [1] });
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
  });

  test('pagination is honored', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/api/posts?page=1&pageSize=1');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.data.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);
    expect(res.body.pagination.page).toBe(1);
    expect(res.body.pagination.pageSize).toBe(1);
  });

  test('filter my_posts requires auth', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/api/posts?filter=my_posts');
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
  });

  test('filter my_posts works when authed', async () => {
    const res = await agent.get('/api/posts?filter=my_posts');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.data.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  test('filter my_likes works when authed', async () => {
    const res = await agent.get('/api/posts?filter=my_likes');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.data.length).toBe(0); // no likes yet
  });

  test('unknown category returns 404', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/api/posts?category=NoSuchCategory');
    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
  });
});


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\tests\reactions.test.js ===
require('./setup');
const { resetDb, getCsrfFor, request } = require('./helpers');

let app, agent, csrf, postId;

beforeAll(async () => {
  await resetDb();
  app = require('../src/app');
  agent = request.agent(app);
  const { token } = await getCsrfFor(agent);
  csrf = token;

  await agent.post('/register').set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
    .send({ username: 'ruser', email: 'r@example.com', password: 'longpassword' });
  await agent.post('/login').set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
    .send({ username: 'ruser', password: 'longpassword' });

  // Title must be at least 3 chars (validator requires 3-150).
  const p = await agent.post('/api/posts').set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
    .send({ title: 'Reaction test post', content: 'Body content', categoryIds: [1] });
  postId = p.body.id;
});

describe('Reactions', () => {
  test('like without CSRF is 403', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).post(`/api/posts/${postId}/like`);
    expect(res.status).toBe(403);
  });

  test('like without auth (after CSRF) is 401', async () => {
    const anonAgent = request.agent(app);
    const { token } = await getCsrfFor(anonAgent);
    const res = await anonAgent
      .post(`/api/posts/${postId}/like`)
      .set('x-csrf-token', token);
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
  });

  test('like adds reaction', async () => {
    const res = await agent
      .post(`/api/posts/${postId}/like`)
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.action).toBe('added');
    expect(res.body.value).toBe(1);
  });

  test('second like removes it (toggle)', async () => {
    const res = await agent
      .post(`/api/posts/${postId}/like`)
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.action).toBe('removed');
  });

  test('dislike after like changes value', async () => {
    await agent.post(`/api/posts/${postId}/like`).set('x-csrf-token', csrf);

    const res = await agent
      .post(`/api/posts/${postId}/dislike`)
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.action).toBe('changed');
    expect(res.body.value).toBe(-1);
  });

  test('unique constraint: one reaction per (user, target)', async () => {
    const db = require('../src/database/db');
    const count = db.prepare(
      "SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM reactions WHERE target_type='post' AND target_id=?"
    ).get(postId).c;
    expect(count).toBe(1);
  });

  test('like on comment works', async () => {
    const c = await agent.post(`/api/posts/${postId}/comments`)
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
      .send({ content: 'Nice comment' });
    const commentId = c.body.id;

    const res = await agent
      .post(`/api/comments/${commentId}/like`)
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf);
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.action).toBe('added');
  });
});


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\tests\security.test.js ===
require('./setup');
const { resetDb, getCsrfFor, request } = require('./helpers');

let app, agent, csrf;

beforeAll(async () => {
  await resetDb();
  app = require('../src/app');
  agent = request.agent(app);
  const { token } = await getCsrfFor(agent);
  csrf = token;

  await agent.post('/register').set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
    .send({ username: 'suser', email: 's@example.com', password: 'longpassword' });
  await agent.post('/login').set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
    .send({ username: 'suser', password: 'longpassword' });
});

describe('Security', () => {
  test('session cookie is HttpOnly and SameSite=Lax', async () => {
    // Fresh agent so the CSRF cookie and header match.
    const freshAgent = request.agent(app);
    const { token } = await getCsrfFor(freshAgent);

    await freshAgent
      .post('/register')
      .set('x-csrf-token', token)
      .send({
        username: 'cookieuser',
        email: 'cookieuser@e.com',
        password: 'longpassword',
      });

    const res = await freshAgent
      .post('/login')
      .set('x-csrf-token', token)
      .send({ username: 'cookieuser', password: 'longpassword' });

    const cookies = res.headers['set-cookie'] || [];
    const sess = cookies.find((c) => c.startsWith('forum_session='));
    expect(sess).toBeDefined();
    expect(sess).toMatch(/HttpOnly/i);
    expect(sess).toMatch(/SameSite=Lax/i);
  });

  test('body limit rejects oversized payload', async () => {
    const big = 'x'.repeat(50000); // > 10kb
    const res = await agent
      .post('/api/posts')
      .set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
      .send({ title: 'A', content: big, categoryIds: [1] });
    expect([400, 413]).toContain(res.status);
  });

  test('unknown route returns JSON 404', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/api/nonexistent');
    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
    expect(res.body.error).toBe('Not found');
  });

  test('SQL injection in category is not exploitable', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get(
      "/api/posts?category=' OR 1=1 --"
    );
    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
  });

  test('security headers are set', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/auth/providers');
    expect(res.headers['x-content-type-options']).toBe('nosniff');
    expect(res.headers['x-frame-options']).toBe('DENY');
    expect(res.headers['content-security-policy']).toBeDefined();
  });

  test('error responses do not leak stack in production', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/api/posts?filter=my_posts');
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
    expect(res.body.error).toBeDefined();
  });
});

=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\tests\sso.github.test.js ===
require('./setup');

jest.mock('../src/auth/providers/github.provider', () => ({
  buildAuthUrl: jest.fn(({ state, codeChallenge }) => {
    const u = new URL('https://github.com/login/oauth/authorize');
    u.searchParams.set('state', state);
    u.searchParams.set('code_challenge', codeChallenge);
    u.searchParams.set('code_challenge_method', 'S256');
    return u.toString();
  }),
  exchangeCode: jest.fn(async () => ({
    provider: 'github',
    providerUserId: 'gh-uid-42',
    email: 'bob.github@example.com',
    emailVerified: true,
    displayName: 'Bob GitHub',
    avatarUrl: 'https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/42',
  })),
}));

process.env.GITHUB_CLIENT_ID = 'test-gh-id';
process.env.GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET = 'test-gh-secret';
process.env.GITHUB_REDIRECT_URI = 'https://localhost:3000/auth/github/callback';

const { resetDb, request } = require('./helpers');

let app;

beforeAll(async () => {
  await resetDb();
  app = require('../src/app');
});

describe('SSO — GitHub', () => {
  test('status shows github enabled', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/auth/providers');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.providers.github).toBe(true);
  });

  test('start redirects to GitHub with PKCE', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/auth/github');
    expect(res.status).toBe(302);
    const u = new URL(res.headers.location);
    expect(u.origin + u.pathname).toBe('https://github.com/login/oauth/authorize');
    expect(u.searchParams.get('code_challenge_method')).toBe('S256');
    expect(u.searchParams.get('state')).toBeTruthy();
  });

  test('callback rejects bad state', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/auth/github/callback?code=x&state=nope');
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
  });

  test('full flow creates user + session', async () => {
    const agent = request.agent(app);
    const s = await agent.get('/auth/github');
    const state = new URL(s.headers.location).searchParams.get('state');

    const cb = await agent.get(`/auth/github/callback?code=ok&state=${state}`);
    expect(cb.status).toBe(302);

    const me = await agent.get('/me');
    expect(me.status).toBe(200);
    expect(me.body.loggedIn).toBe(true);

    const db = require('../src/database/db');
    const row = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM identities WHERE provider='github'").get();
    expect(row.provider_user_id).toBe('gh-uid-42');
  });

  test('unknown provider returns 404', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/auth/twitter');
    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
  });
});


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\tests\sso.google.test.js ===
require('./setup');

jest.mock('../src/auth/providers/google.provider', () => ({
  buildAuthUrl: jest.fn(async ({ state, nonce, codeChallenge }) => {
    const u = new URL('https://accounts.google.com/o/oauth2/v2/auth');
    u.searchParams.set('state', state);
    u.searchParams.set('nonce', nonce);
    u.searchParams.set('code_challenge', codeChallenge);
    u.searchParams.set('code_challenge_method', 'S256');
    return u.toString();
  }),
  exchangeCode: jest.fn(async () => ({
    provider: 'google',
    providerUserId: 'google-uid-1',
    email: 'alice.google@example.com',
    emailVerified: true,
    displayName: 'Alice Google',
    avatarUrl: 'https://example.com/a.png',
  })),
}));

process.env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID = 'test-google-id';
process.env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET = 'test-google-secret';
process.env.GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI = 'https://localhost:3000/auth/google/callback';

const { resetDb, request } = require('./helpers');

let app;

beforeAll(async () => {
  await resetDb();
  app = require('../src/app');
});

describe('SSO — Google', () => {
  test('GET /auth/providers reports google enabled', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/auth/providers');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.providers.google).toBe(true);
  });

  test('GET /auth/google redirects with PKCE params', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/auth/google');
    expect(res.status).toBe(302);
    const loc = res.headers.location;
    expect(loc).toMatch(/^https:\/\/accounts\.google\.com/);
    const u = new URL(loc);
    expect(u.searchParams.get('code_challenge_method')).toBe('S256');
    expect(u.searchParams.get('state')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(u.searchParams.get('nonce')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(u.searchParams.get('code_challenge')).toBeTruthy();
  });

  test('callback rejects unknown state', async () => {
    const res = await request(app).get('/auth/google/callback?code=x&state=bogus');
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
    expect(res.body.error).toMatch(/state/i);
  });

  test('full flow: creates user + session', async () => {
    const agent = request.agent(app);

    const start = await agent.get('/auth/google');
    const state = new URL(start.headers.location).searchParams.get('state');

    const cb = await agent.get(`/auth/google/callback?code=ok&state=${state}`);
    expect(cb.status).toBe(302);

    const me = await agent.get('/me');
    expect(me.status).toBe(200);
    expect(me.body.loggedIn).toBe(true);

    const db = require('../src/database/db');
    const ids = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM identities WHERE provider = 'google'").all();
    expect(ids.length).toBe(1);
    expect(ids[0].email).toBe('alice.google@example.com');
  });

  test('state is single-use (replay fails)', async () => {
    const agent = request.agent(app);
    const s = await agent.get('/auth/google');
    const state = new URL(s.headers.location).searchParams.get('state');

    const first = await agent.get(`/auth/google/callback?code=ok&state=${state}`);
    expect(first.status).toBe(302);

    const replay = await agent.get(`/auth/google/callback?code=ok&state=${state}`);
    expect(replay.status).toBe(400);
  });
});


=== File: C:\dev\forum-project\tests\sso.linking.test.js ===
require('./setup');

jest.mock('../src/auth/providers/google.provider', () => ({
  buildAuthUrl: jest.fn(async ({ state }) =>
    `https://accounts.google.com/x?state=${state}`),
  exchangeCode: jest.fn(async () => ({
    provider: 'google',
    providerUserId: 'goog-A',
    email: 'shared@example.com',
    emailVerified: true,
    displayName: 'Google User',
    avatarUrl: null,
  })),
}));

jest.mock('../src/auth/providers/github.provider', () => ({
  buildAuthUrl: jest.fn(({ state }) =>
    `https://github.com/login/oauth/authorize?state=${state}`),
  exchangeCode: jest.fn(async () => ({
    provider: 'github',
    providerUserId: 'gh-A',
    email: 'shared@example.com',
    emailVerified: true,
    displayName: 'GitHub User',
    avatarUrl: null,
  })),
}));

process.env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID = 'g-id';
process.env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET = 'g-secret';
process.env.GITHUB_CLIENT_ID = 'h-id';
process.env.GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET = 'h-secret';

const { resetDb, getCsrfFor, request } = require('./helpers');

let app;

beforeAll(async () => {
  await resetDb();
  app = require('../src/app');
});

describe('SSO — Account Linking', () => {
  test('verified email links to existing local account', async () => {
    const agent = request.agent(app);
    const { token: csrf } = await getCsrfFor(agent);

    const reg = await agent.post('/register').set('x-csrf-token', csrf)
      .send({ username: 'shareduser', email: 'shared@example.com', password: 'longpassword' });
    expect(reg.status).toBe(201);

    const db = require('../src/database/db');
    const before = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM users').get().c;

    const s = await agent.get('/auth/google');
    const state = new URL(s.headers.location).searchParams.get('state');
    const cb = await agent.get(`/auth/google/callback?code=ok&state=${state}`);
    expect(cb.status).toBe(302);

    const after = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM users').get().c;
    expect(after).toBe(before);

    const identity = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM identities WHERE provider='google'").get();
    expect(identity).toBeTruthy();
    expect(identity.email).toBe('shared@example.com');
  });

  test('user can accumulate multiple providers', async () => {
    const db = require('../src/database/db');

    const agent = request.agent(app);
    const s = await agent.get('/auth/github');
    const st = new URL(s.headers.location).searchParams.get('state');
    const cb = await agent.get(`/auth/github/callback?code=ok&state=${st}`);
    expect(cb.status).toBe(302);

    const userId = db.prepare("SELECT user_id FROM identities WHERE provider='google'").get().user_id;
    const linked = db.prepare(
      'SELECT provider FROM identities WHERE user_id = ? ORDER BY provider'
    ).all(userId).map((r) => r.provider);

    expect(linked).toEqual(['github', 'google']);
  });
});

describe('SSO — Unverified Email Protection', () => {
  test('unverified email does not link to existing user', async () => {
    const googleProvider = require('../src/auth/providers/google.provider');
    googleProvider.exchangeCode.mockImplementationOnce(async () => ({
      provider: 'google',
      providerUserId: 'goog-attacker',
      email: 'shared@example.com',
      emailVerified: false,
      displayName: 'Attacker',
      avatarUrl: null,
    }));

    const db = require('../src/database/db');
    const before = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM users').get().c;

    const agent = request.agent(app);
    const s = await agent.get('/auth/google');
    const state = new URL(s.headers.location).searchParams.get('state');
    const cb = await agent.get(`/auth/google/callback?code=ok&state=${state}`);

    expect(cb.status).toBe(409);

    const after = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM users').get().c;
    expect(after).toBe(before);

    const attackerIdentity = db.prepare(
      "SELECT * FROM identities WHERE provider_user_id = 'goog-attacker'"
    ).get();
    expect(attackerIdentity).toBeUndefined();
  });
});
